# ProvenanceService

ProvenanceService is the provenance tracking contract: the session/turn journal, generation fencing, checkpoint phases, frozen-envelope storage, the session ledger and the provenance projection [`provenance_messages.proto#L1-L3`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L1-L3). It replaces the legacy agent journal protocol and the `atomic agent database-owner` command, which are being dismantled so that atomicd is the only storage owner [`provenance_messages.proto#L5-L12`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L5-L12). Both proto files are marked DRAFT, add-only [`provenance_service.proto#L1-L3`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L1-L3).

The 18 RPCs fall into six groups:

- **Hook log** (AppendHookLog, LoadHookLog): session-scoped operational diagnostics kept outside the running-turn provenance journal [`provenance_service.proto#L13`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L13) [`provenance_messages.proto#L58`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L58).
- **Journal** (ReserveTurn, AppendEnvelopes, PrepareCheckpoint, LoadFrozenEnvelopes, BindCheckpointHash, AcknowledgeCheckpoint, UpdateTurn, GetTurn): the legacy 13-op journal surface folded into 8 RPCs [`provenance_messages.proto#L44-L48`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L44-L48). Each handler maps its request onto the shared `provenance_core` functions behind the per-repository gate [`services_provenance.rs#L1-L15`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L1-L15).
- **Session ledger** (GetSession, ForkSession, RebuildSessionIndex, ListSessions): `atomic session show/fork/rebuild` and the recent-session listing [`provenance_service.proto#L118`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L118) [`provenance_service.proto#L174-L175`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L174-L175).
- **Projection** (GetProvenance, ExportProvenance): `atomic provenance show/trace` [`provenance_service.proto#L141`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L141).
- **Typed turn dispatch** (DispatchTurnEvent): a transitional seam where the daemon hosts the turn orchestrator in-process [`provenance_service.proto#L160-L166`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L160-L166).
- **Turn reasoning** (ExplainTurns): `atomic agent explain` [`provenance_service.proto#L176-L184`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L176-L184).

Implementation status at this commit: 17 of the 18 RPCs have handlers. GetProvenance returns `UNIMPLEMENTED` [`services_agent.rs#L4841-L4846`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4841-L4846). ExportProvenance is partial: it never fills `prov_jsonld` [`services_agent.rs#L5351-L5355`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5351-L5355). The `ProvenanceImpl` trait implementation delegates the hook-log and journal RPCs to `services_provenance.rs` and GetSession to `services_query.rs`, and implements the rest itself [`services_agent.rs#L4482-L4496`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4482-L4496) [`services_agent.rs#L4628-L4689`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4628-L4689).

**Service-wide behaviour.** The points below apply to every method unless its section says otherwise.

<a id="repository-gate"></a>
- **One request at a time per repository.** Each RPC acquires the repository's gate before it opens the database. In this slice the gate serializes every request, reads included [`state.rs#L35-L44`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L35-L44) [`state.rs#L56-L58`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L56-L58). All 17 handlers take it, for example [`services_provenance.rs#L87-L88`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L87-L88) and [`services_agent.rs#L4703-L4704`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4703-L4704). The proto README instead says read-only operations run as concurrent read transactions [`README.md#L114-L117`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/README.md?plain=1#L114-L117).

<a id="repository-reference"></a>
- **`repository` is always needed.** The two hook-log handlers reject a missing `repository` with a plain `INVALID_ARGUMENT` "repository required", without the `atomic:` code prefix [`services_provenance.rs#L81-L84`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L81-L84). Every other handler calls `request.repository.as_ref().unwrap()` with no check, for example [`services_provenance.rs#L430`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L430), so a request without it panics inside the handler (see Open questions). Resolution accepts only authority `local` and a registered `repository_id` [`state.rs#L331-L348`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L331-L348).

<a id="request-meta"></a>
- **RequestMeta and ResponseMeta.** Where a response has `meta` (field 100), the handler builds it with `response_meta`, which copies `request_id` and sets `replayed = false` and no `snapshot`. When the request has no `meta`, the response has none either [`services_agent.rs#L1233-L1239`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L1233-L1239). No handler in this service validates `request_id`, records it or looks up replay state, so `IDEMPOTENCY_MISMATCH` is never returned and `replayed` is never true. The contract requires both [`CONTRACT.md#L54-L58`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/CONTRACT.md?plain=1#L54-L58) [`common.proto#L8-L17`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/common/common.proto#L8-L17) [`provenance_messages.proto#L19-L20`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L19-L20). Retry safety instead comes from each operation's own semantics: ReserveTurn returns the existing reservation [`provenance_core.rs#L106-L108`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L106-L108), AppendEnvelopes re-acknowledges duplicate event IDs [`provenance_core.rs#L15-L16`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L15-L16), a retried BindCheckpointHash rebuilds the identical session turn [`services_provenance.rs#L280-L286`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L280-L286), and re-appending the same hook-log event returns its original sequence [`hook_health_dispatch.rs#L261-L266`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/tests/hook_health_dispatch.rs#L261-L266). See [RequestMeta](../types.md#requestmeta) and [ResponseMeta](../types.md#responsemeta).

<a id="caller-supplied-time"></a>
- **Caller-supplied time.** The journal handlers take "now" from `meta.observed_at`. Only its `seconds` are used, and when `meta` or `observed_at` is absent the handlers use 0 [`services_provenance.rs#L155-L163`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L155-L163). The handlers never substitute the server clock for observation times [`provenance_core.rs#L13-L14`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L13-L14). Every timestamp they return carries whole seconds with `nanos = 0` [`services_provenance.rs#L165-L167`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L165-L167). AcknowledgeCheckpoint takes its time from `completed_at` instead [`services_provenance.rs#L773-L776`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L773-L776). DispatchTurnEvent is an exception: it falls back to the server clock when the event has no timestamp [`services_agent.rs#L4563-L4567`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4563-L4567).

<a id="generation-fencing"></a>
- **Generation fencing.** The proto describes it this way: ReserveTurn grants the generation, every later mutation carries `expected_generation`, bind and acknowledge advance to `attempt_generation`, and a mismatch is `GENERATION_CONFLICT` [`provenance_messages.proto#L15-L18`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L15-L18). In libatomic the fence is applied by the store, and its error text ("generation is stale") is passed through verbatim [`provenance_core.rs#L11-L12`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L11-L12). The error is classified like any other store failure for that RPC: `PROVENANCE_STORE` for reserve and append, `PROVENANCE_CHECKPOINT` for prepare, load, bind and acknowledge, and `PROVENANCE_LIFECYCLE` for the lifecycle RPCs [`services_provenance.rs#L13-L15`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L13-L15). No handler emits `GENERATION_CONFLICT`. The in-process journal sink shows which generation goes where. AppendEnvelopes and PrepareCheckpoint get the reservation's generation [`journal_sink.rs#L183-L190`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/journal_sink.rs#L183-L190) [`journal_sink.rs#L207-L213`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/journal_sink.rs#L207-L213). BindCheckpointHash and AcknowledgeCheckpoint get the prepared attempt's `attempt_generation` [`journal_sink.rs#L242-L249`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/journal_sink.rs#L242-L249) [`journal_sink.rs#L264-L269`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/journal_sink.rs#L264-L269). LoadFrozenEnvelopes takes `attempt_generation` from the PrepareCheckpoint response [`provenance_messages.proto#L245`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L245). Resuming a turn assigns a new generation [`provenance_core.rs#L390`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L390). UpdateTurn's `expected_generation` is checked only for being nonzero ([UpdateTurn](#updateturn)).

<a id="checkpoint-sequence"></a>
- **Checkpoint sequence and crash-injection seams.** A turn moves through reserve → append → prepare → load → bind → acknowledge. The named crash-injection failpoints sit on exactly those seams, which is why prepare, bind and acknowledge stay separate RPCs [`provenance_messages.proto#L23-L28`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L23-L28) [`CONTRACT.md#L217`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/CONTRACT.md?plain=1#L217). The failpoints are before-envelope-commit, after-envelope-commit, after-checkpoint-prepare, before-frozen-page-continuation, after-checkpoint-bind, and the frozen-page-unavailable injected error [`provenance_core.rs#L32-L43`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L32-L43). A tripped failpoint aborts the daemon process. The legacy test suite relies on the retry converging afterwards [`provenance_core.rs#L85-L104`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L85-L104).

<a id="errors-convention"></a>
- **Errors.** Domain refusals are built with `domain_status`, which maps the ErrorCode to a gRPC status. `PROVENANCE_STORE`, `PROVENANCE_CHECKPOINT`, `PROVENANCE_LIFECYCLE` and `CHANGES` fall through to `INVALID_ARGUMENT` [`state.rs#L432-L446`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L432-L446). The code travels as an `atomic:<CODE>: ` prefix on the status message [`state.rs#L447-L452`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L447-L452). See [how handlers produce errors](../errors.md#how-handlers-produce-errors) and [ErrorCode](../errors.md#errorcode). Every handler maps a failed blocking task to a plain `INTERNAL`, for example [`services_provenance.rs#L107-L108`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L107-L108).

<a id="sandbox-callers"></a>
- **Sandbox callers.** libatomic carries no transport. A request that carries neither a host-attached grant nor `x-atomic-sandbox-token-bin` metadata is treated as a local, trusted caller [`sandbox_grants.rs#L9-L21`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/sandbox_grants.rs#L9-L21). Only the eight journal handlers resolve a sandbox caller. They check its capability and target, re-validate the grant after waiting for the gate, and check its turn or session scope, for example [`services_provenance.rs#L428-L445`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L428-L445) [`services_provenance.rs#L451-L458`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L451-L458). The scope rules are in [`services_provenance.rs#L333-L346`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L333-L346). GetSession, GetProvenance and ExportProvenance are on the sandbox allowlist [`sandbox_grants.rs#L64-L88`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/sandbox_grants.rs#L64-L88) but not on the list of methods whose handlers enforce a grant's scope. The host is told to refuse a sandbox principal on every method outside that list [`sandbox_grants.rs#L105-L127`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/sandbox_grants.rs#L105-L127). For non-sandbox principals the handlers check no capability. Method-option enforcement for them happens outside these handlers; see [authentication and authorization](../transport.md#authentication-and-authorization).

<a id="localservice"></a>
- **LocalService.** No ProvenanceService method is routed by the in-process `LocalService`. An unknown method falls through to `UNIMPLEMENTED` "local method is not supported" [`local.rs#L358-L360`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/local.rs#L358-L360). See [LocalService](../transport.md#in-process-localservice).

<a id="getcapabilities"></a>
- **GetCapabilities.** METHOD_MATRIX lists 13 of the 18 methods. ForkSession, RebuildSessionIndex, GetProvenance, ExportProvenance and ExplainTurns are absent [`services.rs#L97-L109`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services.rs#L97-L109). AppendHookLog and LoadHookLog are advertised with descriptors that match their method options [`services.rs#L183-L203`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services.rs#L183-L203). The other 11 are advertised with scope BOTH and empty capabilities, callers and effects [`services.rs#L204-L212`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services.rs#L204-L212).

## Methods

### AppendHookLog

`rpc AppendHookLog(AppendHookLogRequest) returns (AppendHookLogResponse)`. Unary [`provenance_service.proto#L14`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L14).

Appends one operational diagnostic event, such as a hook dispatch outcome, to the repository's session log. This log is kept outside the running-turn provenance journal [`provenance_service.proto#L13`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L13) [`provenance_messages.proto#L58`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L58). The handler copies the event field for field into the store's session-log event and appends it. It then flushes any events spooled to disk earlier [`services_provenance.rs#L89-L101`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L89-L101), and returns the sequence the store assigned [`services_provenance.rs#L102-L105`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L102-L105).

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|
| Execution scope | `EXECUTION_SCOPE_LOCAL` [`provenance_service.proto#L15`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L15) ([method options](../types.md#method-options), [ExecutionScope](../types.md#executionscope)) |
| Allowed caller | `CALLER_CLASS_LOCAL` [`provenance_service.proto#L17`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L17) ([CallerClass](../types.md#callerclass)) |
| Effect | `RPC_EFFECT_REPOSITORY_WRITE` [`provenance_service.proto#L18`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L18) ([RpcEffect](../types.md#rpceffect)) |
| Required capability | `provenance.write` [`provenance_service.proto#L16`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L16) ([required capability](../types.md#required-capability)) |
| Implemented | yes [`services_agent.rs#L4484-L4489`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4484-L4489) [`services_provenance.rs#L76-L110`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L76-L110) |
| In-process `LocalService` | not routed [`local.rs#L358-L360`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/local.rs#L358-L360) ([LocalService](../transport.md#in-process-localservice)) |
| Advertised by GetCapabilities | yes [`services.rs#L98`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services.rs#L98), with a LOCAL / `provenance.write` / LOCAL / REPOSITORY_WRITE descriptor [`services.rs#L183-L203`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services.rs#L183-L203) |

**Request**: `AppendHookLogRequest` [`provenance_messages.proto#L70-L74`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L70-L74)

| Field | # | Type | Required | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `repository` | 1 | [RepositoryRef](../types.md#repositoryref) | **Required** | Target repository. If it is absent the handler returns `INVALID_ARGUMENT` "repository required" [`services_provenance.rs#L81-L84`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L81-L84). |
| `meta` | 2 | [RequestMeta](../types.md#requestmeta) | **Optional** | Only echoed: the response `meta` is built from it [`services_provenance.rs#L102-L105`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L102-L105). `observed_at` is not read. |
| `event` | 3 | [HookLogEvent](#hooklogevent) | **Required** | The event to append. If it is absent the handler returns `INVALID_ARGUMENT` "event required" [`services_provenance.rs#L89-L93`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L89-L93). The handler does not validate its fields (see Open questions). |

**Response**: `AppendHookLogResponse` [`provenance_messages.proto#L75-L78`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L75-L78)

| Field | # | Type | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `sequence` | 1 | `uint64` | Session-log sequence assigned to the event. Always set [`services_provenance.rs#L96-L98`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L96-L98). Re-appending the identical event returns the same sequence [`hook_health_dispatch.rs#L261-L262`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/tests/hook_health_dispatch.rs#L261-L262). |
| `meta` | 100 | [ResponseMeta](../types.md#responsemeta) | Set when the request had `meta` [`services_provenance.rs#L104`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L104). |

**Errors**

| gRPC status | ErrorCode | When | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | none (plain status) | `repository` absent | [`services_provenance.rs#L81-L84`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L81-L84) |
| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | none (plain status) | `event` absent | [`services_provenance.rs#L89-L93`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L89-L93) |
| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `repository.authority` is not `local` | [`state.rs#L332-L340`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L332-L340) |
| `NOT_FOUND` | `REPOSITORY_NOT_FOUND` | repository not registered with this daemon | [`state.rs#L341-L347`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L341-L347) |
| `INTERNAL` | `REPOSITORY` | the `.atomic` directory or database path cannot be resolved | [`state.rs#L254-L257`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L254-L257) |
| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `PROVENANCE_STORE` | the change store cannot be opened | [`state.rs#L258-L263`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L258-L263) |
| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `PROVENANCE_STORE` | the store refused the append | [`services_provenance.rs#L96-L98`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L96-L98) |
| `INTERNAL` | none | the blocking task did not complete | [`services_provenance.rs#L107-L108`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L107-L108) |

**Behaviour**

- Retrying is safe. Sending the same event again returns the original sequence and adds no second entry [`hook_health_dispatch.rs#L261-L266`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/tests/hook_health_dispatch.rs#L261-L266).
- `at` is an RFC 3339 time supplied by the caller and kept unchanged across retries [`provenance_messages.proto#L68`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L68).
- If flushing earlier spooled events fails, the handler only logs a warning. The RPC still succeeds [`services_provenance.rs#L99-L101`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L99-L101).

### LoadHookLog

`rpc LoadHookLog(LoadHookLogRequest) returns (LoadHookLogResponse)`. Unary [`provenance_service.proto#L20`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L20).

Reads one page of session-log entries that follow a given sequence, optionally only for one session [`services_provenance.rs#L125-L143`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L125-L143).

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|
| Execution scope | `EXECUTION_SCOPE_LOCAL` [`provenance_service.proto#L21`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L21) ([method options](../types.md#method-options)) |
| Allowed caller | `CALLER_CLASS_LOCAL` [`provenance_service.proto#L23`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L23) |
| Effect | `RPC_EFFECT_REPOSITORY_READ` [`provenance_service.proto#L24`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L24) |
| Required capability | `provenance.read` [`provenance_service.proto#L22`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L22) |
| Implemented | yes [`services_agent.rs#L4491-L4496`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4491-L4496) [`services_provenance.rs#L112-L149`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L112-L149) |
| In-process `LocalService` | not routed [`local.rs#L358-L360`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/local.rs#L358-L360) |
| Advertised by GetCapabilities | yes [`services.rs#L99`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services.rs#L99), with a LOCAL / `provenance.read` / LOCAL / REPOSITORY_READ descriptor [`services.rs#L183-L203`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services.rs#L183-L203) |

**Request**: `LoadHookLogRequest` [`provenance_messages.proto#L79-L85`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L79-L85)

| Field | # | Type | Required | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `repository` | 1 | [RepositoryRef](../types.md#repositoryref) | **Required** | If it is absent the handler returns `INVALID_ARGUMENT` "repository required" [`services_provenance.rs#L117-L120`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L117-L120). |
| `meta` | 2 | [RequestMeta](../types.md#requestmeta) | **Ignored** | The handler reads only `session_id`, `after_sequence` and `limit` [`services_provenance.rs#L125-L136`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L125-L136). The response has no `meta` field [`provenance_messages.proto#L90-L92`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L90-L92). |
| `session_id` | 3 | `optional string` | **Optional** | When set, only that session's events are returned. When absent, events from all sessions [`services_provenance.rs#L128-L129`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L128-L129). |
| `after_sequence` | 4 | `uint64` | **Optional** | Exclusive lower bound. 0 means from the start [`services_provenance.rs#L130`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L130). To continue, pass the last returned entry's sequence [`hook_health_dispatch.rs#L232-L236`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/tests/hook_health_dispatch.rs#L232-L236). |
| `limit` | 5 | `uint32` | **Optional** | 0 means 512. Any other value is passed through unchanged [`services_provenance.rs#L131-L135`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L131-L135). The proto says the limit is bounded to 512 [`provenance_messages.proto#L84`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L84). |

**Response**: `LoadHookLogResponse` [`provenance_messages.proto#L90-L92`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L90-L92)

| Field | # | Type | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `entries` | 1 | `repeated HookLogEntry` | The page. Each entry is `{sequence = 1 (uint64), event = 2 (`[HookLogEvent](#hooklogevent)`)}` [`provenance_messages.proto#L86-L89`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L86-L89), with both fields always set [`services_provenance.rs#L139-L142`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L139-L142). |

**Errors**

| gRPC status | ErrorCode | When | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | none (plain status) | `repository` absent | [`services_provenance.rs#L117-L120`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L117-L120) |
| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `repository.authority` is not `local` | [`state.rs#L332-L340`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L332-L340) |
| `NOT_FOUND` | `REPOSITORY_NOT_FOUND` | repository not registered | [`state.rs#L341-L347`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L341-L347) |
| `INTERNAL` | `REPOSITORY` | the database path cannot be resolved | [`state.rs#L254-L257`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L254-L257) |
| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `PROVENANCE_STORE` | the change store cannot be opened | [`state.rs#L258-L263`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L258-L263) |
| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `PROVENANCE_STORE` | the store failed to read the log | [`services_provenance.rs#L127-L137`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L127-L137) |
| `INTERNAL` | none | the blocking task did not complete | [`services_provenance.rs#L146-L147`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L146-L147) |

**Behaviour**

- No cursor or end marker is returned. Paging works by passing the last entry's `sequence` as the next `after_sequence`, as the test does with `limit = 5` [`hook_health_dispatch.rs#L230-L236`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/tests/hook_health_dispatch.rs#L230-L236).
- The handler takes the [repository gate](#repository-gate) although it only reads [`services_provenance.rs#L123-L124`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L123-L124).

### ReserveTurn

`rpc ReserveTurn(ReserveTurnRequest) returns (ReserveTurnResponse)`. Unary [`provenance_service.proto#L28`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L28).

Grants the generation for a session turn. The first reserve claims the session for the caller's view [`provenance_service.proto#L26-L27`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L26-L27). The handler reserves the stable identity of `(session_id, turn_number)`, or returns it if it already exists. A committed reservation is returned only after the store's transaction commits [`provenance_core.rs#L106-L118`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L106-L118). For a sandbox caller, the reserved turn is also bound to the sandbox's grant, so no other sandbox can use that turn's envelopes, checkpoint or acknowledgement [`services_provenance.rs#L462-L468`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L462-L468). The turn-scope check runs only for sandbox callers [`services_provenance.rs#L509-L516`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L509-L516) [`services_provenance.rs#L576-L578`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L576-L578) [`services_provenance.rs#L718-L720`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L718-L720) [`services_provenance.rs#L780-L782`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L780-L782); a request with neither a grant nor a token is a trusted local caller [`sandbox_grants.rs#L19-L20`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/sandbox_grants.rs#L19-L20), and it can append to, prepare, bind or acknowledge a sandbox's turn.

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|
| Execution scope | `EXECUTION_SCOPE_BOTH` [`provenance_service.proto#L29`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L29) ([method options](../types.md#method-options)) |
| Allowed caller | `CALLER_CLASS_LOCAL`, `CALLER_CLASS_REMOTE_IDENTITY`, `CALLER_CLASS_SANDBOX` [`provenance_service.proto#L31-L33`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L31-L33) |
| Effect | `RPC_EFFECT_REPOSITORY_WRITE` [`provenance_service.proto#L34`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L34) |
| Required capability | `agent.checkpoint` [`provenance_service.proto#L30`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L30). The handler checks it only for sandbox callers [`services_provenance.rs#L432-L433`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L432-L433). |
| Implemented | yes [`services_agent.rs#L4628-L4633`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4628-L4633) [`services_provenance.rs#L424-L479`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L424-L479) |
| In-process `LocalService` | not routed [`local.rs#L358-L360`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/local.rs#L358-L360) |
| Advertised by GetCapabilities | yes [`services.rs#L100`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services.rs#L100), with an empty descriptor [`services.rs#L204-L212`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services.rs#L204-L212) |

**Request**: `ReserveTurnRequest` [`provenance_messages.proto#L204-L210`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L204-L210)

| Field | # | Type | Required | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `repository` | 1 | [RepositoryRef](../types.md#repositoryref) | **Required** | Unwrapped without a check [`services_provenance.rs#L430`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L430). A sandbox request must name the grant's repository and no `workspace_id` [`sandbox_grants.rs#L515-L526`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/sandbox_grants.rs#L515-L526). |
| `meta` | 2 | [RequestMeta](../types.md#requestmeta) | **Optional** | `observed_at.seconds` is the reservation's "now", or 0 when absent [`services_provenance.rs#L447`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L447) [`services_provenance.rs#L158-L163`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L158-L163). `request_id` is echoed [`services_provenance.rs#L470-L474`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L470-L474). |
| `session_id` | 3 | `string` | **Unconfirmed** | Passed unchecked to the store [`services_provenance.rs#L448`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L448) [`services_provenance.rs#L459-L461`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L459-L461). A sandbox may use only a session its view started, or one nothing has been recorded for [`services_provenance.rs#L375-L383`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L375-L383). |
| `turn_number` | 4 | `uint32` | **Unconfirmed** | Passed unchecked to the store [`services_provenance.rs#L449`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L449) [`services_provenance.rs#L459-L461`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L459-L461). |
| `view` | 5 | [ViewRef](../types.md#viewref) | **Optional** | The proto calls it required [`provenance_messages.proto#L209`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L209). The handler reads it only for sandbox callers: if present, its name or `view_id` must match the grant's view [`services_provenance.rs#L432-L439`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L432-L439) [`sandbox_grants.rs#L527-L539`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/sandbox_grants.rs#L527-L539). A sandbox reserve without `view` succeeds in the tests [`sandbox_service_test.rs#L1243-L1249`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/tests/sandbox_service_test.rs#L1243-L1249) [`sandbox_service_test.rs#L1269`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/tests/sandbox_service_test.rs#L1269). Local callers' `view` is not read. |

**Response**: `ReserveTurnResponse` [`provenance_messages.proto#L211-L215`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L211-L215)

| Field | # | Type | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `turn` | 1 | [StoredProvenanceTurn](#storedprovenanceturn) | The reserved turn, carrying the granted generation [`provenance_messages.proto#L212`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L212). Always set [`services_provenance.rs#L470-L471`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L470-L471). A repeated reserve of the same turn returns the existing reservation [`provenance_core.rs#L106-L108`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L106-L108). |
| `committed` | 2 | `bool` | Always `true`. The store returns only after its transaction commits [`services_provenance.rs#L469-L472`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L469-L472). |
| `meta` | 100 | [ResponseMeta](../types.md#responsemeta) | Set when the request had `meta` [`services_provenance.rs#L473`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L473). |

**Errors**

| gRPC status | ErrorCode | When | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `UNAUTHENTICATED` | `UNAUTHORIZED` | sandbox credential refused: more than one token, a token beside `authorization`, malformed metadata, or an unknown, revoked or expired token | [`sandbox_grants.rs#L573-L605`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/sandbox_grants.rs#L573-L605) [`sandbox_grants.rs#L213-L221`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/sandbox_grants.rs#L213-L221) |
| `PERMISSION_DENIED` | `FORBIDDEN` | the sandbox grant lacks `agent.checkpoint` | [`services_provenance.rs#L433`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L433) [`sandbox_grants.rs#L496-L504`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/sandbox_grants.rs#L496-L504) |
| `PERMISSION_DENIED` | `FORBIDDEN` | the sandbox request names a workspace, another repository or another view | [`services_provenance.rs#L434-L438`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L434-L438) [`sandbox_grants.rs#L509-L541`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/sandbox_grants.rs#L509-L541) |
| `UNAUTHENTICATED` | `UNAUTHORIZED` | the grant expired or was revoked while the request waited for the gate | [`services_provenance.rs#L443-L445`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L443-L445) [`sandbox_grants.rs#L545-L555`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/sandbox_grants.rs#L545-L555) |
| `PERMISSION_DENIED` | `FORBIDDEN` | "that session is not this sandbox's": a session owned by another view, or one that already has recorded turns but no sandbox owner | [`services_provenance.rs#L375-L383`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L375-L383) [`sandbox_grants.rs#L442-L458`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/sandbox_grants.rs#L442-L458) |
| `INTERNAL` | `INTERNAL` | the host's grant store failed | [`sandbox_grants.rs#L213-L221`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/sandbox_grants.rs#L213-L221) |
| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `repository.authority` is not `local` | [`state.rs#L332-L340`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L332-L340) |
| `NOT_FOUND` | `REPOSITORY_NOT_FOUND` | repository not registered | [`state.rs#L341-L347`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L341-L347) |
| `INTERNAL` | `REPOSITORY` | the database path cannot be resolved | [`state.rs#L254-L257`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L254-L257) |
| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `PROVENANCE_STORE` | the change store cannot be opened, or the sandbox's session-use lookup failed | [`state.rs#L258-L263`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L258-L263) [`services_provenance.rs#L366-L372`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L366-L372) |
| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `PROVENANCE_STORE` | the store refused the reservation | [`provenance_core.rs#L115-L117`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L115-L117) |
| `INTERNAL` | none | the blocking task did not complete | [`services_provenance.rs#L476-L477`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L476-L477) |

**Behaviour**

- Send the returned `turn.provenance_id` and `turn.generation` with AppendEnvelopes and PrepareCheckpoint. The in-process sink keeps exactly that pair as the reservation [`journal_sink.rs#L158-L161`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/journal_sink.rs#L158-L161) and passes it to append and prepare [`journal_sink.rs#L183-L190`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/journal_sink.rs#L183-L190) [`journal_sink.rs#L207-L213`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/journal_sink.rs#L207-L213).
- Sandbox ownership is kept per repository and view. A reissued token for the same view keeps its sessions, and a token for another view never gains them [`sandbox_grants.rs#L265-L267`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/sandbox_grants.rs#L265-L267). In the default in-memory grant store the owner is the grant's repository ID and view *name*; the grant's internal `view_id` [`sandbox_grants.rs#L149-L151`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/sandbox_grants.rs#L149-L151) is not part of it [`sandbox_grants.rs#L309-L318`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/sandbox_grants.rs#L309-L318) [`sandbox_grants.rs#L346-L348`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/sandbox_grants.rs#L346-L348), and the turn-scope checks compare only that owner [`services_provenance.rs#L373-L415`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L373-L415). The store only ever adds owner entries: closing a sandbox removes its grants but not its sessions or turns [`sandbox_grants.rs#L420-L427`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/sandbox_grants.rs#L420-L427) [`sandbox_grants.rs#L442-L479`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/sandbox_grants.rs#L442-L479). A grant issued for a view that was deleted and recreated under the same name therefore inherits the old view's sessions and turns, contrary to the contract [`CONTRACT.md#L159-L164`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/CONTRACT.md?plain=1#L159-L164); see [Open questions](#open-questions).

### AppendEnvelopes

`rpc AppendEnvelopes(AppendEnvelopesRequest) returns (AppendEnvelopesResponse)`. Unary [`provenance_service.proto#L40`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L40).

Appends a batch of journal envelopes to a reserved turn. Clients chunk the batch themselves, and each acknowledgement carries the event's sequence [`provenance_service.proto#L37-L39`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L37-L39). The batch is fenced by generation. Duplicate event IDs re-acknowledge their original sequences, and a committed batch returns only after the transaction commits [`provenance_core.rs#L120-L136`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L120-L136).

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|
| Execution scope | `EXECUTION_SCOPE_BOTH` [`provenance_service.proto#L41`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L41) ([method options](../types.md#method-options)) |
| Allowed caller | `CALLER_CLASS_LOCAL`, `CALLER_CLASS_REMOTE_IDENTITY`, `CALLER_CLASS_SANDBOX` [`provenance_service.proto#L43-L45`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L43-L45) |
| Effect | `RPC_EFFECT_REPOSITORY_WRITE` [`provenance_service.proto#L46`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L46) |
| Required capability | `provenance.write` [`provenance_service.proto#L42`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L42). Checked only for sandbox callers [`services_provenance.rs#L489-L490`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L489-L490). |
| Implemented | yes [`services_agent.rs#L4635-L4640`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4635-L4640) [`services_provenance.rs#L481-L545`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L481-L545) |
| In-process `LocalService` | not routed [`local.rs#L358-L360`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/local.rs#L358-L360) |
| Advertised by GetCapabilities | yes [`services.rs#L101`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services.rs#L101), with an empty descriptor [`services.rs#L204-L212`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services.rs#L204-L212) |

**Request**: `AppendEnvelopesRequest` [`provenance_messages.proto#L217-L223`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L217-L223)

| Field | # | Type | Required | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `repository` | 1 | [RepositoryRef](../types.md#repositoryref) | **Required** | Unwrapped without a check [`services_provenance.rs#L487`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L487). |
| `meta` | 2 | [RequestMeta](../types.md#requestmeta) | **Optional** | `observed_at.seconds` is the append's "now", or 0 when absent [`services_provenance.rs#L500`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L500). `request_id` is echoed [`services_provenance.rs#L537-L540`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L537-L540). |
| `provenance_id` | 3 | `uint64` | **Required** | The turn from ReserveTurn; the in-process sink keeps the reserved ID and sends it with each append [`journal_sink.rs#L158-L161`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/journal_sink.rs#L158-L161) [`journal_sink.rs#L183-L190`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/journal_sink.rs#L183-L190). Passed unchecked to the store [`services_provenance.rs#L501`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L501) [`services_provenance.rs#L522-L528`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L522-L528). A sandbox may only append to a turn its view reserved [`services_provenance.rs#L509-L516`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L509-L516) [`services_provenance.rs#L384-L388`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L384-L388). |
| `expected_generation` | 4 | `uint64` | **Required** | The generation fence [`provenance_messages.proto#L221`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L221). libatomic fences every journal mutation on it [`provenance_core.rs#L11-L12`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L11-L12), and the proto calls a mismatch a conflict, never a silent race [`provenance_messages.proto#L15-L18`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L15-L18). Passed unchecked to the store, which applies the fence [`services_provenance.rs#L502`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L502) [`services_provenance.rs#L522-L528`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L522-L528). |
| `envelopes` | 5 | `repeated WireEnvelope` | **Optional** | The batch. An empty batch succeeds [`sandbox_service_test.rs#L1288-L1300`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/tests/sandbox_service_test.rs#L1288-L1300). The handler keeps only `event_id` and `envelope` of each entry [`services_provenance.rs#L503-L507`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L503-L507). |

`WireEnvelope` [`provenance_messages.proto#L169-L176`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L169-L176):

| Field | # | Type | Required | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `event_id` | 1 | `string` | **Unconfirmed** | Event identity within the turn. Appends are idempotent by event ID [`provenance_core.rs#L15-L16`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L15-L16). |
| `type_url` | 2 | `string` | **Ignored** | Dropped by the handler [`services_provenance.rs#L503-L507`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L503-L507). In the proto it is the schema/type URL that aligns this envelope with the Reactor event envelope [`provenance_messages.proto#L169-L171`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L169-L171). |
| `envelope` | 3 | `bytes` | **Unconfirmed** | The envelope bytes, stored as given [`services_provenance.rs#L506`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L506). |

**Response**: `AppendEnvelopesResponse` [`provenance_messages.proto#L224-L227`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L224-L227)

| Field | # | Type | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `acknowledgements` | 1 | `repeated EnvelopeAck` | One `{event_id = 1, sequence = 2}` per stored event [`provenance_messages.proto#L178-L182`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L178-L182), mapped from the store's event ID and sequence [`services_provenance.rs#L530-L536`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L530-L536). For a duplicate event ID, `sequence` is the original one [`provenance_core.rs#L120-L122`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L120-L122). |
| `meta` | 100 | [ResponseMeta](../types.md#responsemeta) | Set when the request had `meta` [`services_provenance.rs#L537-L540`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L537-L540). |

**Behaviour**

- Chunk large batches on the client under `limits.append_chunk_budget_bytes` [`provenance_service.proto#L37-L39`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L37-L39). GetCapabilities advertises 4 MiB [`services.rs#L230`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services.rs#L230). The handler enforces no size limit of its own [`services_provenance.rs#L503-L529`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L503-L529).
- Retrying a batch is safe: the batch is re-acknowledged with the stored sequences, with no duplicate events [`provenance_core.rs#L15-L16`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L15-L16).
- The before-envelope-commit and after-envelope-commit failpoints bracket the store call [`provenance_core.rs#L130-L134`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L130-L134).

### PrepareCheckpoint

`rpc PrepareCheckpoint(PrepareCheckpointRequest) returns (PrepareCheckpointResponse)`. Unary [`provenance_service.proto#L52`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L52).

Freezes envelopes for checkpointing [`provenance_service.proto#L49-L51`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L49-L51). The handler freezes the journal frontier and persists one recoverable checkpoint attempt [`provenance_core.rs#L138-L143`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L138-L143). The returned attempt's `attempt_generation` and `frozen_event_count` are what LoadFrozenEnvelopes takes [`provenance_messages.proto#L245-L246`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L245-L246).

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|
| Execution scope | `EXECUTION_SCOPE_BOTH` [`provenance_service.proto#L53`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L53) ([method options](../types.md#method-options)) |
| Allowed caller | `CALLER_CLASS_LOCAL`, `CALLER_CLASS_REMOTE_IDENTITY`, `CALLER_CLASS_SANDBOX` [`provenance_service.proto#L55-L57`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L55-L57) |
| Effect | `RPC_EFFECT_REPOSITORY_WRITE` [`provenance_service.proto#L58`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L58) |
| Required capability | `agent.checkpoint` [`provenance_service.proto#L54`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L54). Checked only for sandbox callers [`services_provenance.rs#L555-L556`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L555-L556). |
| Implemented | yes [`services_agent.rs#L4642-L4647`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4642-L4647) [`services_provenance.rs#L547-L603`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L547-L603) |
| In-process `LocalService` | not routed [`local.rs#L358-L360`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/local.rs#L358-L360) |
| Advertised by GetCapabilities | yes [`services.rs#L102`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services.rs#L102), with an empty descriptor [`services.rs#L204-L212`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services.rs#L204-L212) |

**Request**: `PrepareCheckpointRequest` [`provenance_messages.proto#L229-L236`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L229-L236)

| Field | # | Type | Required | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `repository` | 1 | [RepositoryRef](../types.md#repositoryref) | **Required** | Unwrapped without a check [`services_provenance.rs#L553`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L553). |
| `meta` | 2 | [RequestMeta](../types.md#requestmeta) | **Optional** | `observed_at.seconds` is the prepare's "now", or 0 when absent [`services_provenance.rs#L566`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L566) [`services_provenance.rs#L586-L593`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L586-L593). `request_id` is echoed [`services_provenance.rs#L595-L598`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L595-L598). |
| `provenance_id` | 3 | `uint64` | **Required** | The turn from ReserveTurn; the in-process sink sends the reserved ID [`journal_sink.rs#L207-L213`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/journal_sink.rs#L207-L213). Passed unchecked to the store [`services_provenance.rs#L567`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L567). A sandbox may only prepare a turn its view reserved [`services_provenance.rs#L389-L392`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L389-L392). |
| `expected_generation` | 4 | `uint64` | **Required** | The turn's current generation (from ReserveTurn, or from UpdateTurn after a resume) [`journal_sink.rs#L207-L213`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/journal_sink.rs#L207-L213). Fenced by the store on every journal mutation [`provenance_core.rs#L11-L12`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L11-L12). |
| `source` | 5 | [CheckpointSource](#checkpointsource) | **Required** | If it is absent the handler returns `INVALID_ARGUMENT` "checkpoint source is required" [`services_provenance.rs#L570-L574`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L570-L574). |
| `reuse_frozen_changes` | 6 | `bool` | **Optional** | Default `false`. When `true` and `source.change_hashes` is empty, the handler reuses the stored attempt's change hashes, but only if the stored turn's generation equals `expected_generation` and the turn is CHECKPOINTING [`provenance_core.rs#L152-L163`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L152-L163). |

**Response**: `PrepareCheckpointResponse` [`provenance_messages.proto#L237-L240`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L237-L240)

| Field | # | Type | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `attempt` | 1 | [ProvenanceCheckpointAttempt](#provenancecheckpointattempt) | The persisted attempt. Always set [`services_provenance.rs#L595-L596`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L595-L596). |
| `meta` | 100 | [ResponseMeta](../types.md#responsemeta) | Set when the request had `meta` [`services_provenance.rs#L597`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L597). |
