**Behaviour**

- `reuse_frozen_changes` handles one recovery case. A previous Stop may have recorded the change before its journal read failed, so the retry sees a clean working copy. Only the frozen hashes are recovered, and the store's source validation still applies: a new nonempty change set must still conflict [`provenance_core.rs#L17-L21`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L17-L21).
- In `source`, a `change_hashes` entry or a `previous_provenance` whose value is not exactly 32 bytes is silently dropped, not rejected [`services_provenance.rs#L260-L278`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L260-L278).
- For a sandbox, only `source.change_hashes` is checked against the grant's view [`services_provenance.rs#L575`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L575) [`services_provenance.rs#L389-L406`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L389-L406). The contract also requires checks on previous provenance and plan links [`CONTRACT.md#L169-L171`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/CONTRACT.md?plain=1#L169-L171).
- The after-checkpoint-prepare failpoint follows the store call [`provenance_core.rs#L167`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L167).

### LoadFrozenEnvelopes

`rpc LoadFrozenEnvelopes(LoadFrozenEnvelopesRequest) returns (LoadFrozenEnvelopesResponse)`. Unary [`provenance_service.proto#L65`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L65).

Loads frozen envelopes as pages. With no cursor it returns the first page, and a complete journal may arrive as a single page [`provenance_service.proto#L61-L64`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L61-L64). The handler reads one bounded part of the frozen journal of a prepared checkpoint [`provenance_core.rs#L191-L202`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L191-L202). Frozen loads are paged only; the legacy unpaged form is not carried [`provenance_messages.proto#L29-L34`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L29-L34).

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|
| Execution scope | `EXECUTION_SCOPE_BOTH` [`provenance_service.proto#L66`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L66) ([method options](../types.md#method-options)) |
| Allowed caller | `CALLER_CLASS_LOCAL`, `CALLER_CLASS_REMOTE_IDENTITY`, `CALLER_CLASS_SANDBOX` [`provenance_service.proto#L68-L70`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L68-L70) |
| Effect | `RPC_EFFECT_REPOSITORY_READ` [`provenance_service.proto#L71`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L71) |
| Required capability | `provenance.read` [`provenance_service.proto#L67`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L67). Checked only for sandbox callers [`services_provenance.rs#L613-L614`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L613-L614). |
| Implemented | yes [`services_agent.rs#L4649-L4654`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4649-L4654) [`services_provenance.rs#L605-L682`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L605-L682) |
| In-process `LocalService` | not routed [`local.rs#L358-L360`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/local.rs#L358-L360) |
| Advertised by GetCapabilities | yes [`services.rs#L103`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services.rs#L103), with an empty descriptor [`services.rs#L204-L212`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services.rs#L204-L212) |

**Request**: `LoadFrozenEnvelopesRequest` [`provenance_messages.proto#L242-L249`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L242-L249). It has no `meta` field.

| Field | # | Type | Required | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `repository` | 1 | [RepositoryRef](../types.md#repositoryref) | **Required** | Unwrapped without a check [`services_provenance.rs#L611`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L611). |
| `provenance_id` | 2 | `uint64` | **Required** | The checkpointed turn; the in-process sink reads by the attempt's turn ID [`journal_sink.rs#L228-L230`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/journal_sink.rs#L228-L230). Passed unchecked to the store [`services_provenance.rs#L623`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L623) [`services_provenance.rs#L647-L656`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L647-L656). A sandbox may only read a turn its view reserved [`services_provenance.rs#L639-L646`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L639-L646). |
| `attempt_generation` | 3 | `uint64` | **Unconfirmed** | From the PrepareCheckpoint response [`provenance_messages.proto#L245`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L245). Passed unchecked to the store [`services_provenance.rs#L624`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L624). |
| `frozen_event_count` | 4 | `uint64` | **Unconfirmed** | Exclusive journal-sequence cutoff, from the attempt [`provenance_messages.proto#L246`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L246). Passed unchecked to the store [`services_provenance.rs#L625`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L625). |
| `cursor` | 5 | `optional` [PageCursor](../types.md#pagecursor) | **Optional** | Where to continue. Absent means `{seq: 0, offset: 0}` [`services_provenance.rs#L626-L632`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L626-L632). |
| `budget` | 6 | `optional` [PageBudget](../types.md#pagebudget) | **Optional** | Only `max_bytes` is read; `max_items` is ignored [`services_provenance.rs#L633-L637`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L633-L637). The page byte budget is `min(max_bytes, frozen_page_budget())`, or `frozen_page_budget()` when `max_bytes` is absent [`provenance_core.rs#L206-L208`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L206-L208). |

**Response**: `LoadFrozenEnvelopesResponse` [`provenance_messages.proto#L250-L253`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L250-L253)

| Field | # | Type | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `page` | 1 | [FrozenPage](#frozenpage) | Always set, and `page.next` is always set too [`services_provenance.rs#L669-L677`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L669-L677). |

**Errors**

| gRPC status | ErrorCode | When | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `UNAUTHENTICATED` | `UNAUTHORIZED` | sandbox credential refused | [`services_provenance.rs#L609`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L609) [`sandbox_grants.rs#L573-L605`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/sandbox_grants.rs#L573-L605) |
| `PERMISSION_DENIED` | `FORBIDDEN` | the sandbox grant lacks `provenance.read` | [`services_provenance.rs#L614`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L614) [`sandbox_grants.rs#L496-L504`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/sandbox_grants.rs#L496-L504) |
| `PERMISSION_DENIED` | `FORBIDDEN` | the sandbox request names a workspace or another repository | [`services_provenance.rs#L615`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L615) [`sandbox_grants.rs#L515-L526`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/sandbox_grants.rs#L515-L526) |
| `UNAUTHENTICATED` | `UNAUTHORIZED` | the grant expired or was revoked while waiting for the gate | [`services_provenance.rs#L620-L622`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L620-L622) [`sandbox_grants.rs#L545-L555`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/sandbox_grants.rs#L545-L555) |
| `PERMISSION_DENIED` | `FORBIDDEN` | "that provenance turn is not this sandbox's" | [`services_provenance.rs#L384-L388`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L384-L388) |
| `INTERNAL` | `INTERNAL` | the host's grant store failed | [`sandbox_grants.rs#L213-L221`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/sandbox_grants.rs#L213-L221) |
| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `repository.authority` is not `local` | [`state.rs#L332-L340`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L332-L340) |
| `NOT_FOUND` | `REPOSITORY_NOT_FOUND` | repository not registered | [`state.rs#L341-L347`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L341-L347) |
| `INTERNAL` | `REPOSITORY` | the database path cannot be resolved | [`state.rs#L254-L257`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L254-L257) |
| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `PROVENANCE_STORE` | the change store cannot be opened | [`state.rs#L258-L263`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L258-L263) |
| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `PROVENANCE_CHECKPOINT` | the store refused the read | [`provenance_core.rs#L209-L215`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L209-L215) [`provenance_core.rs#L227`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L227) |
| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `PROVENANCE_CHECKPOINT` | "injected frozen page read failure" (frozen-page-unavailable failpoint) | [`provenance_core.rs#L216-L224`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L216-L224) |
| `INTERNAL` | none | the blocking task did not complete | [`services_provenance.rs#L679-L680`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L679-L680) |

**Behaviour**

- **Budget.** `frozen_page_budget()` reserves the empty-response frame overhead and the worst-case fragment metadata out of the 64 MiB frame limit, divides the rest by four and clamps it to the 1 MiB page size [`provenance_core.rs#L184-L189`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L184-L189) [`provenance_core.rs#L55-L68`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L55-L68). A unit test asserts the result is 1 MiB [`provenance_core.rs#L444-L447`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L444-L447). A page holds at most 256 fragments [`provenance_core.rs#L450-L453`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L450-L453). GetCapabilities advertises the same limits [`services.rs#L227-L230`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services.rs#L227-L230).
- **End of the journal.** The proto says an absent `next` marks the end [`provenance_messages.proto#L195`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L195). The handler always sets `next` [`services_provenance.rs#L672-L675`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L672-L675). The end is the cursor `{seq: frozen_event_count, offset: 0}`. The in-process loader keeps paging while `cursor.seq < frozen_event_count` [`provenance_core.rs#L296`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L296), and the reassembler treats that cursor as the end [`provenance_core.rs#L242-L246`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L242-L246).
- **Reassembly.** One envelope larger than the page budget spans fragments, and the client reassembles them with strict continuity validation [`provenance_messages.proto#L184-L186`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L184-L186). The reference algorithm, `append_frozen_page`, is the one the legacy owner client used [`provenance_core.rs#L231-L234`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L231-L234). A Go client must apply the same rules to each page:
  - `page.next` must be greater than the current cursor and no greater than `{frozen_event_count, 0}` [`provenance_core.rs#L242-L248`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L242-L248).
  - For each fragment: `cursor.seq` must be below the cutoff, and the cursor must not go back. A fragment may jump forward only from an envelope boundary to the start of an envelope, both offsets 0, since sequence gaps are allowed between envelopes but never inside one. A fragment that is not `complete` must carry bytes [`provenance_core.rs#L249-L259`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L249-L259).
  - Append the fragment's bytes to the pending envelope. A `complete` fragment ends it and moves the position to `{seq + 1, 0}`; otherwise the position becomes `{seq, bytes so far}` [`provenance_core.rs#L260-L273`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L260-L273).
  - After the last fragment, `page.next` must not be behind the position. It may differ from the position only between envelopes, and no partial envelope may remain when `page.next` is the end [`provenance_core.rs#L274-L279`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L274-L279).
  - Advance the cursor to `page.next` only after the page validates, so a retried RPC never appends its bytes twice [`provenance_core.rs#L231-L232`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L231-L232) [`provenance_core.rs#L280-L281`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L280-L281).
- A request whose cursor is not the default passes the before-frozen-page-continuation failpoint [`provenance_core.rs#L203-L205`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L203-L205).

### BindCheckpointHash

`rpc BindCheckpointHash(BindCheckpointHashRequest) returns (BindCheckpointHashResponse)`. Unary [`provenance_service.proto#L77`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L77).

Binds a hash to the prepared checkpoint attempt [`provenance_service.proto#L74-L76`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L74-L76). The core binds the prepared graph hash and the exact immutable session turn once [`provenance_core.rs#L320-L334`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L320-L334). The wire `SessionTurn` carries only `(session_id, turn_number)`. The handler enriches it from the prepared attempt's frozen source (change hashes, previous provenance, plan id) and uses the attempt's `prepared_at` as the timestamp. A retried bind therefore rebuilds the identical session turn and reaches the store's idempotent path. The goal and todos fields cannot be carried over the wire [`services_provenance.rs#L280-L319`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L280-L319). The request's `hash` becomes the session turn's `provenance_hash` [`services_provenance.rs#L294-L299`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L294-L299).

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|
| Execution scope | `EXECUTION_SCOPE_BOTH` [`provenance_service.proto#L78`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L78) ([method options](../types.md#method-options)) |
| Allowed caller | `CALLER_CLASS_LOCAL`, `CALLER_CLASS_REMOTE_IDENTITY`, `CALLER_CLASS_SANDBOX` [`provenance_service.proto#L80-L82`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L80-L82) |
| Effect | `RPC_EFFECT_REPOSITORY_WRITE` [`provenance_service.proto#L83`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L83) |
| Required capability | `agent.checkpoint` [`provenance_service.proto#L79`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L79). Checked only for sandbox callers [`services_provenance.rs#L692-L693`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L692-L693). |
| Implemented | yes [`services_agent.rs#L4656-L4661`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4656-L4661) [`services_provenance.rs#L684-L752`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L684-L752) |

| Advertised by GetCapabilities | yes [`services.rs#L104`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services.rs#L104), with an empty descriptor [`services.rs#L204-L212`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services.rs#L204-L212) |

**Request**: `BindCheckpointHashRequest` [`provenance_messages.proto#L255-L262`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L255-L262)

| Field | # | Type | Required | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `repository` | 1 | [RepositoryRef](../types.md#repositoryref) | **Required** | Unwrapped without a check [`services_provenance.rs#L690`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L690). |
| `meta` | 2 | [RequestMeta](../types.md#requestmeta) | **Optional** | `observed_at.seconds` is the bind's "now", or 0 when absent [`services_provenance.rs#L703`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L703). The session turn's timestamp also falls back to it when no prepared attempt is stored [`services_provenance.rs#L301`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L301) [`services_provenance.rs#L308-L316`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L308-L316). `request_id` is echoed [`services_provenance.rs#L744-L747`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L744-L747). |
| `provenance_id` | 3 | `uint64` | **Required** | The checkpointed turn; the in-process sink binds by the attempt's turn ID [`journal_sink.rs#L242-L249`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/journal_sink.rs#L242-L249). Passed unchecked to the store [`services_provenance.rs#L704`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L704) [`services_provenance.rs#L735-L742`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L735-L742). A sandbox may only bind a turn its view reserved [`services_provenance.rs#L719-L726`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L719-L726). |
| `expected_generation` | 4 | `uint64` | **Required** | Fenced by the store on every journal mutation [`provenance_core.rs#L11-L12`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L11-L12). The in-process sink passes the attempt's `attempt_generation` here [`journal_sink.rs#L242-L249`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/journal_sink.rs#L242-L249). |
| `hash` | 5 | [Hash](../types.md#hash) | **Required** | The proto calls it the manifest hash [`provenance_messages.proto#L260`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L260). The handler uses it as the session turn's `provenance_hash` [`services_provenance.rs#L298`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L298). It is rejected if absent, if not exactly 32 bytes [`services_provenance.rs#L249-L258`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L249-L258) [`services_provenance.rs#L707`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L707), or if its algorithm is neither BLAKE3 nor unspecified [`services_provenance.rs#L708-L713`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L708-L713) [`services_provenance.rs#L325-L327`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L325-L327). |
| `session_turn` | 6 | [SessionTurn](../types.md#sessionturn) | **Required** | If it is absent the handler returns `INVALID_ARGUMENT` "session turn is required" [`services_provenance.rs#L714-L717`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L714-L717). Only `session_id` and `turn_number` are taken from it [`services_provenance.rs#L294-L296`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L294-L296). |

**Response**: `BindCheckpointHashResponse` [`provenance_messages.proto#L263-L266`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L263-L266)

| Field | # | Type | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `attempt` | 1 | [ProvenanceCheckpointAttempt](#provenancecheckpointattempt) | The attempt after binding. Always set [`services_provenance.rs#L744-L745`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L744-L745). |
| `meta` | 100 | [ResponseMeta](../types.md#responsemeta) | Set when the request had `meta` [`services_provenance.rs#L746`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L746). |

| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `PROVENANCE_STORE` | the change store cannot be opened | [`state.rs#L258-L263`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L258-L263) |
| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `PROVENANCE_CHECKPOINT` | the store refused the bind, including a stale generation | [`provenance_core.rs#L329-L331`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L329-L331) [`provenance_core.rs#L11-L12`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L11-L12) |
| `INTERNAL` | none | the blocking task did not complete | [`services_provenance.rs#L749-L750`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L749-L750) |

**Behaviour**

- The hash checks run before the sandbox scope check and before the store is opened [`services_provenance.rs#L706-L726`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L706-L726).
- The contract requires that a bind refers to the prepared turn and attempt and never replaces another SessionTurn [`CONTRACT.md#L170-L171`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/CONTRACT.md?plain=1#L170-L171).
- The after-checkpoint-bind failpoint follows the store call [`provenance_core.rs#L332`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L332).

### AcknowledgeCheckpoint

`rpc AcknowledgeCheckpoint(AcknowledgeCheckpointRequest) returns (AcknowledgeCheckpointResponse)`. Unary [`provenance_service.proto#L88`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L88).

Completes the turn: the checkpoint is acknowledged with a completion time supplied by the caller [`provenance_service.proto#L86-L87`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L86-L87). The core acknowledges publication and completes the turn [`provenance_core.rs#L336-L348`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L336-L348).

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|
| Execution scope | `EXECUTION_SCOPE_BOTH` [`provenance_service.proto#L89`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L89) ([method options](../types.md#method-options)) |
| Allowed caller | `CALLER_CLASS_LOCAL`, `CALLER_CLASS_REMOTE_IDENTITY`, `CALLER_CLASS_SANDBOX` [`provenance_service.proto#L91-L93`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L91-L93) |
| Effect | `RPC_EFFECT_REPOSITORY_WRITE` [`provenance_service.proto#L94`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L94) |
| Required capability | `agent.checkpoint` [`provenance_service.proto#L90`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L90). Checked only for sandbox callers [`services_provenance.rs#L762-L763`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L762-L763). |
| Implemented | yes [`services_agent.rs#L4663-L4668`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4663-L4668) [`services_provenance.rs#L754-L805`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L754-L805) |
| In-process `LocalService` | not routed [`local.rs#L358-L360`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/local.rs#L358-L360) |
| Advertised by GetCapabilities | yes [`services.rs#L105`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services.rs#L105), with an empty descriptor [`services.rs#L204-L212`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services.rs#L204-L212) |

**Request**: `AcknowledgeCheckpointRequest` [`provenance_messages.proto#L268-L275`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L268-L275)

| Field | # | Type | Required | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `repository` | 1 | [RepositoryRef](../types.md#repositoryref) | **Required** | Unwrapped without a check [`services_provenance.rs#L760`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L760). |
| `meta` | 2 | [RequestMeta](../types.md#requestmeta) | **Optional** | Only `request_id` is used, and only for the echo [`services_provenance.rs#L772`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L772) [`services_provenance.rs#L798-L800`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L798-L800). `observed_at` is not read; the time comes from `completed_at`. |
| `provenance_id` | 3 | `uint64` | **Required** | The checkpointed turn; the in-process sink acknowledges by the attempt's turn ID [`journal_sink.rs#L264-L269`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/journal_sink.rs#L264-L269). Passed unchecked to the store [`services_provenance.rs#L777`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L777) [`services_provenance.rs#L790-L796`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L790-L796). A sandbox may only acknowledge a turn its view reserved [`services_provenance.rs#L781-L788`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L781-L788). |
| `expected_generation` | 4 | `uint64` | **Required** | Fenced by the store on every journal mutation [`provenance_core.rs#L11-L12`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L11-L12). The in-process sink passes the attempt's `attempt_generation` here [`journal_sink.rs#L264-L269`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/journal_sink.rs#L264-L269). |
| `manifest_hash` | 5 | [Hash](../types.md#hash) | **Required** | Rejected if absent or not exactly 32 bytes [`services_provenance.rs#L779`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L779) [`services_provenance.rs#L249-L258`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L249-L258). Its algorithm is not checked. |
| `completed_at` | 6 | `google.protobuf.Timestamp` | **Required** | Caller-supplied completion time, never the server clock. Only `seconds` is used. If it is absent the handler returns `INVALID_ARGUMENT` "completed_at is required" [`services_provenance.rs#L773-L776`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L773-L776). |

|---|---|---|---|

| gRPC status | ErrorCode | When | Source |
|---|---|---|---|

| `PERMISSION_DENIED` | `FORBIDDEN` | the sandbox request names a workspace or another repository | [`services_provenance.rs#L764`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L764) [`sandbox_grants.rs#L515-L526`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/sandbox_grants.rs#L515-L526) |
| `UNAUTHENTICATED` | `UNAUTHORIZED` | the grant expired or was revoked while waiting for the gate | [`services_provenance.rs#L769-L771`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L769-L771) [`sandbox_grants.rs#L545-L555`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/sandbox_grants.rs#L545-L555) |
| `PERMISSION_DENIED` | `FORBIDDEN` | "that provenance turn is not this sandbox's" | [`services_provenance.rs#L384-L388`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L384-L388) |

| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `PROVENANCE_STORE` | the change store cannot be opened | [`state.rs#L258-L263`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L258-L263) |
| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `PROVENANCE_CHECKPOINT` | the store refused the acknowledgement, including a stale generation | [`provenance_core.rs#L344-L346`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L344-L346) [`provenance_core.rs#L11-L12`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L11-L12) |
| `INTERNAL` | none | the blocking task did not complete | [`services_provenance.rs#L802-L803`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L802-L803) |

**Behaviour**

- No failpoint sits on the acknowledgement [`provenance_core.rs#L336-L348`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L336-L348).

### UpdateTurn

`rpc UpdateTurn(UpdateTurnRequest) returns (UpdateTurnResponse)`. Unary [`provenance_service.proto#L99`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L99).

Stops, resumes or abandons the turn `(session_id, turn_number)`, selected by `action`. No crash-injection seams sit on these transitions [`provenance_service.proto#L97-L98`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L97-L98) [`services_provenance.rs#L856-L889`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L856-L889). STOP stops a running turn [`provenance_core.rs#L360-L371`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L360-L371). RESUME resumes a stopped turn with a new fencing generation and the same ID [`provenance_core.rs#L390-L404`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L390-L404). ABANDON seals a pending turn without publishing a checkpoint, with cause ABANDONED and `resumable = false` [`provenance_core.rs#L406-L429`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L406-L429).

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|
| Execution scope | `EXECUTION_SCOPE_BOTH` [`provenance_service.proto#L100`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L100) ([method options](../types.md#method-options)) |
| Allowed caller | `CALLER_CLASS_LOCAL`, `CALLER_CLASS_REMOTE_IDENTITY`, `CALLER_CLASS_SANDBOX` [`provenance_service.proto#L102-L104`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L102-L104) |
| Effect | `RPC_EFFECT_REPOSITORY_WRITE` [`provenance_service.proto#L105`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L105) |
| Required capability | `provenance.write` [`provenance_service.proto#L101`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L101). Checked only for sandbox callers [`services_provenance.rs#L815-L816`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L815-L816). |
| Implemented | yes [`services_agent.rs#L4670-L4675`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4670-L4675) [`services_provenance.rs#L807-L898`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L807-L898) |
| In-process `LocalService` | not routed [`local.rs#L358-L360`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/local.rs#L358-L360) |
| Advertised by GetCapabilities | yes [`services.rs#L106`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services.rs#L106), with an empty descriptor [`services.rs#L204-L212`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services.rs#L204-L212) |

**Request**: `UpdateTurnRequest` [`provenance_messages.proto#L287-L296`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L287-L296)

| Field | # | Type | Required | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `repository` | 1 | [RepositoryRef](../types.md#repositoryref) | **Required** | Unwrapped without a check [`services_provenance.rs#L813`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L813). |
| `meta` | 2 | [RequestMeta](../types.md#requestmeta) | **Optional** | `observed_at` is the lifecycle observation time [`provenance_messages.proto#L289`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L289). Its `seconds` become the stop or abandon `observed_at`, or the resume time, and 0 is used when it is absent [`services_provenance.rs#L826`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L826) [`services_provenance.rs#L874-L886`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L874-L886). `request_id` is echoed [`services_provenance.rs#L890-L893`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L890-L893). |
| `session_id` | 3 | `string` | **Required** | Lookup key, passed unchecked to the store [`services_provenance.rs#L827`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L827) [`provenance_core.rs#L350-L358`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L350-L358). A sandbox may only update its own session, or one nothing has been recorded for [`services_provenance.rs#L408-L415`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L408-L415). With no matching turn, nothing changes and the response has no `turn` [`provenance_core.rs#L372-L374`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L372-L374) [`services_provenance.rs#L890-L893`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L890-L893). |
| `turn_number` | 4 | `uint32` | **Required** | Lookup key, passed unchecked to the store [`services_provenance.rs#L828`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L828) [`provenance_core.rs#L350-L358`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L350-L358). With no matching turn, nothing changes and the response has no `turn` [`provenance_core.rs#L372-L374`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L372-L374) [`services_provenance.rs#L890-L893`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L890-L893). |
| `action` | 5 | `TurnAction` | **Required** | One of `TURN_ACTION_STOP` (1), `TURN_ACTION_RESUME` (2), `TURN_ACTION_ABANDON` (3) [`provenance_messages.proto#L280-L285`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L280-L285). An unknown value gives "unknown turn action", and `TURN_ACTION_UNSPECIFIED` gives "turn action is required" [`services_provenance.rs#L829-L836`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L829-L836). |
| `cause` | 6 | `optional` [StopCause](#stopcause) | **Required** | Required when `action` is STOP: an absent, unknown or `STOP_CAUSE_UNSPECIFIED` value gives "stop cause is required" [`services_provenance.rs#L857-L872`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L857-L872). RESUME and ABANDON do not read it [`services_provenance.rs#L883-L886`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L883-L886). |
| `resumable` | 7 | `optional bool` | **Optional** | Default `false` [`services_provenance.rs#L873`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L873). Only STOP reads it [`services_provenance.rs#L874-L881`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L874-L881). |
| `expected_generation` | 8 | `uint64` | **Required** | Must be nonzero ("expected_generation is required and must be nonzero") [`services_provenance.rs#L840-L845`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L840-L845). Its value is not used for fencing: the transition uses the looked-up turn's own current generation [`services_provenance.rs#L837-L839`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L837-L839) [`provenance_core.rs#L360-L363`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L360-L363) [`provenance_core.rs#L375-L378`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L375-L378). |

**Response**: `UpdateTurnResponse` [`provenance_messages.proto#L297-L300`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L297-L300)

| Field | # | Type | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `turn` | 1 | [StoredProvenanceTurn](#storedprovenanceturn) | The turn after the transition. Absent when no turn is reserved for `(session_id, turn_number)`, which is not an error [`provenance_core.rs#L372-L374`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L372-L374) [`services_provenance.rs#L890-L891`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L890-L891). |
| `meta` | 100 | [ResponseMeta](../types.md#responsemeta) | Set when the request had `meta` [`services_provenance.rs#L892`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L892). |

| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `repository.authority` is not `local` | [`state.rs#L332-L340`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L332-L340) |
| `NOT_FOUND` | `REPOSITORY_NOT_FOUND` | repository not registered | [`state.rs#L341-L347`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L341-L347) |
| `INTERNAL` | `REPOSITORY` | the database path cannot be resolved | [`state.rs#L254-L257`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L254-L257) |
| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `PROVENANCE_STORE` | the change store cannot be opened | [`state.rs#L258-L263`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L258-L263) |
| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `PROVENANCE_LIFECYCLE` | the turn lookup or the transition failed in the store | [`provenance_core.rs#L355-L357`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L355-L357) [`provenance_core.rs#L386`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L386) [`provenance_core.rs#L402`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L402) [`provenance_core.rs#L427`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L427) |
| `INTERNAL` | none | the blocking task did not complete | [`services_provenance.rs#L895-L896`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L895-L896) |

**Behaviour**

- The handler narrows the proto here. The proto says `expected_generation` is required and nonzero, and that a lookup never grants a generation [`provenance_messages.proto#L295`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L295). The contract says lifecycle stop, resume and abandon require `expected_generation` [`CONTRACT.md#L216`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/CONTRACT.md?plain=1#L216). The handler checks only that the value is present and nonzero. The transition always runs against the turn's current generation [`provenance_core.rs#L360-L363`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L360-L363), so UpdateTurn gives no fencing against a concurrent change.
- After RESUME, the returned `turn.generation` is the turn's new fencing generation [`provenance_core.rs#L390`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L390). Send it with the turn's next AppendEnvelopes or PrepareCheckpoint ([generation fencing](#generation-fencing)).

### GetTurn

`rpc GetTurn(GetTurnRequest) returns (GetTurnResponse)`. Unary [`provenance_service.proto#L109`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L109).

Returns the turn's status: the stored journal turn for `(session_id, turn_number)` [`provenance_service.proto#L108`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L108) [`provenance_core.rs#L431-L438`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L431-L438).

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|
| Execution scope | `EXECUTION_SCOPE_BOTH` [`provenance_service.proto#L110`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L110) ([method options](../types.md#method-options)) |
| Allowed caller | `CALLER_CLASS_LOCAL`, `CALLER_CLASS_REMOTE_IDENTITY`, `CALLER_CLASS_SANDBOX` [`provenance_service.proto#L112-L114`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L112-L114) |
| Effect | `RPC_EFFECT_REPOSITORY_READ` [`provenance_service.proto#L115`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L115) |
| Required capability | `provenance.read` [`provenance_service.proto#L111`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L111). Checked only for sandbox callers [`services_provenance.rs#L908-L909`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L908-L909). |
| Implemented | yes [`services_agent.rs#L4677-L4682`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4677-L4682) [`services_provenance.rs#L900-L938`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L900-L938) |
| In-process `LocalService` | not routed [`local.rs#L358-L360`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/local.rs#L358-L360) |
| Advertised by GetCapabilities | yes [`services.rs#L107`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services.rs#L107), with an empty descriptor [`services.rs#L204-L212`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services.rs#L204-L212) |

**Request**: `GetTurnRequest` [`provenance_messages.proto#L302-L306`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L302-L306). It has no `meta` field.

| Field | # | Type | Required | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `repository` | 1 | [RepositoryRef](../types.md#repositoryref) | **Required** | Unwrapped without a check [`services_provenance.rs#L906`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L906). |
| `session_id` | 2 | `string` | **Required** | Lookup key, passed unchecked to the store [`services_provenance.rs#L918`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L918) [`services_provenance.rs#L930`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L930). A sandbox may ask only after its own session, or one nothing has been written to [`services_provenance.rs#L343-L345`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L343-L345) [`services_provenance.rs#L408-L415`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L408-L415). With no matching turn, the response has no `turn` [`services_provenance.rs#L930-L933`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L930-L933). |
| `turn_number` | 3 | `uint32` | **Required** | Lookup key, passed unchecked to the store [`services_provenance.rs#L919`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L919) [`services_provenance.rs#L930`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L930). With no matching turn, the response has no `turn` [`services_provenance.rs#L930-L933`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L930-L933). |

**Response**: `GetTurnResponse` [`provenance_messages.proto#L307-L309`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L307-L309)

| Field | # | Type | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `turn` | 1 | `optional` [StoredProvenanceTurn](#storedprovenanceturn) | The stored turn. Absent when none is reserved for the pair [`services_provenance.rs#L930-L933`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L930-L933). |

| `INTERNAL` | `REPOSITORY` | the database path cannot be resolved | [`state.rs#L254-L257`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L254-L257) |
