**Behaviour**

- A missing turn is not an error: `turn` is simply absent [`services_provenance.rs#L930-L933`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L930-L933).

### GetSession

`rpc GetSession(GetSessionRequest) returns (GetSessionResponse)`. Unary [`provenance_service.proto#L119`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L119).

The session-ledger read behind `atomic session show` [`provenance_service.proto#L118`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L118). It returns the session's recorded turns, the full ledger as versioned bytes, the turn-to-intent resolution, and the session manifest's head and fork data [`services_query.rs#L582-L671`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_query.rs#L582-L671).

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|
| Execution scope | `EXECUTION_SCOPE_BOTH` [`provenance_service.proto#L120`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L120) ([method options](../types.md#method-options)) |
| Allowed caller | `CALLER_CLASS_LOCAL`, `CALLER_CLASS_REMOTE_IDENTITY`, `CALLER_CLASS_SANDBOX` [`provenance_service.proto#L122-L124`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L122-L124) |
| Effect | `RPC_EFFECT_REPOSITORY_READ` [`provenance_service.proto#L125`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L125) |
| Required capability | `provenance.read` [`provenance_service.proto#L121`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L121). The handler checks no capability [`services_query.rs#L582-L593`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_query.rs#L582-L593). |
| Implemented | yes [`services_agent.rs#L4684-L4689`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4684-L4689) [`services_query.rs#L582-L671`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_query.rs#L582-L671) |
| In-process `LocalService` | not routed [`local.rs#L358-L360`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/local.rs#L358-L360) |
| Advertised by GetCapabilities | yes [`services.rs#L108`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services.rs#L108), with an empty descriptor [`services.rs#L204-L212`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services.rs#L204-L212) |

**Request**: `GetSessionRequest` [`provenance_messages.proto#L313-L318`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L313-L318)

| Field | # | Type | Required | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `repository` | 1 | [RepositoryRef](../types.md#repositoryref) | **Required** | Unwrapped without a check [`services_query.rs#L587`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_query.rs#L587). |
| `session_id` | 2 | `string` | **Required** | Lookup key, passed unchecked to the repository [`services_query.rs#L589`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_query.rs#L589) [`services_query.rs#L596-L598`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_query.rs#L596-L598). When no ledger exists for it, the handler returns `NOT_FOUND` [`services_query.rs#L642-L644`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_query.rs#L642-L644). |
| `from_turn` | 3 | `optional uint32` | **Optional** | Inclusive lower bound on the returned `turns`. Default 0 [`services_query.rs#L654-L660`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_query.rs#L654-L660). |
| `to_turn` | 4 | `optional uint32` | **Optional** | Inclusive upper bound on the returned `turns`. Default `u32::MAX` [`services_query.rs#L654-L660`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_query.rs#L654-L660). |

**Response**: `GetSessionResponse` [`provenance_messages.proto#L319-L332`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L319-L332)

| Field | # | Type | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `turns` | 1 | `repeated` [StoredProvenanceTurn](#storedprovenanceturn) | The ledger turns within `[from_turn, to_turn]` [`services_query.rs#L654-L662`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_query.rs#L654-L662). These are ledger turns, not journal turns. Only `session_id`, `turn_number`, `created_at` (the turn timestamp) and `final_hash` (the turn's provenance hash) are filled. `schema_version` is 1, `provenance_id` and `generation` are 0, and `state` is UNSPECIFIED [`services_query.rs#L693-L714`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_query.rs#L693-L714). |
| `ledger_bundle` | 2 | `optional` [VersionedBytes](../types.md#versionedbytes) | Schema `atomic.session.ledger.v1`, the JSON of the `{record, turns}` pair [`provenance_messages.proto#L323-L325`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L323-L325). It is built before the turn filter, so it holds every turn. It is absent only if JSON encoding fails [`services_query.rs#L645-L653`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_query.rs#L645-L653). |
| `turn_intents` | 3 | `repeated SessionTurnIntent` | `{turn = 1 (uint32), intent = 2 (string)}` [`provenance_messages.proto#L334-L338`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L334-L338). One entry for each ledger turn, before the filter, that has an intent: the turn's `plan_id`, or else the vault-manifest intent linked to that session and turn [`services_query.rs#L599-L617`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_query.rs#L599-L617) [`services_query.rs#L673-L691`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_query.rs#L673-L691). |
| `manifest_head` | 4 | `optional` [Hash](../types.md#hash) | The session's head manifest hash, when one exists [`services_query.rs#L618-L619`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_query.rs#L618-L619). |
| `parent_manifest` | 5 | `optional` [Hash](../types.md#hash) | The head manifest's parent-session hash, when set [`services_query.rs#L620-L629`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_query.rs#L620-L629). |
| `fork_turn` | 6 | `optional uint32` | The head manifest's fork turn, when set [`services_query.rs#L620-L629`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_query.rs#L620-L629). |

**Errors**

| gRPC status | ErrorCode | When | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `NOT_FOUND` | `NOT_FOUND` | "session not found" | [`services_query.rs#L642-L644`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_query.rs#L642-L644) |
| `INTERNAL` | `REPOSITORY` | the repository cannot be opened, or the ledger read failed ([how handlers produce errors](../errors.md#how-handlers-produce-errors)) | [`services_query.rs#L595-L598`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_query.rs#L595-L598) [`state.rs#L100-L110`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L100-L110) |
| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `repository.authority` is not `local` | [`state.rs#L332-L340`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L332-L340) |
| `NOT_FOUND` | `REPOSITORY_NOT_FOUND` | repository not registered | [`state.rs#L341-L347`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L341-L347) |
| `INTERNAL` | none | the blocking task did not complete | [`services_query.rs#L638-L639`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_query.rs#L638-L639) |

**Behaviour**

- The proto marks the extra session-stamp fields SHAPE-CONFIRM (Slice 2), to be finalized against `commands/session.rs` [`provenance_messages.proto#L321-L322`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L321-L322).
- If reading the head or the manifest fails, the error is swallowed and the affected fields are left absent [`services_query.rs#L618-L629`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_query.rs#L618-L629).
- The handler does no sandbox checks. GetSession is on the sandbox allowlist [`sandbox_grants.rs#L86`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/sandbox_grants.rs#L86) but not on the list of grant-scoped methods [`sandbox_grants.rs#L105-L127`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/sandbox_grants.rs#L105-L127).
- The handler opens the repository with the writable open, not the read-only one [`services_query.rs#L595`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_query.rs#L595) [`state.rs#L219-L229`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L219-L229). [ListSessions](#listsessions) does the same.

### ForkSession

`rpc ForkSession(ForkSessionRequest) returns (ForkSessionResponse)`. Unary [`provenance_service.proto#L127`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L127).

Backs `atomic session fork`. The fork itself (parent manifest, child session, inherited turns) runs in the handler, and the response carries both manifest hashes for the local fork report [`services_agent.rs#L4786-L4788`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4786-L4788). The child inherits turns `0..=at_turn` [`provenance_messages.proto#L345-L347`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L345-L347).

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|
| Execution scope | `EXECUTION_SCOPE_BOTH` [`provenance_service.proto#L128`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L128) ([method options](../types.md#method-options)) |
| Allowed caller | `CALLER_CLASS_LOCAL`, `CALLER_CLASS_REMOTE_IDENTITY` [`provenance_service.proto#L130-L131`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L130-L131) |
| Effect | `RPC_EFFECT_REPOSITORY_WRITE` [`provenance_service.proto#L132`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L132) |
| Required capability | `provenance.write` [`provenance_service.proto#L129`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L129) |
| Implemented | yes [`services_agent.rs#L4782-L4813`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4782-L4813) |
| In-process `LocalService` | not routed [`local.rs#L358-L360`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/local.rs#L358-L360) |
| Advertised by GetCapabilities | no: absent from METHOD_MATRIX [`services.rs#L97-L109`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services.rs#L97-L109) |

**Request**: `ForkSessionRequest` [`provenance_messages.proto#L340-L348`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L340-L348)

| Field | # | Type | Required | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `repository` | 1 | [RepositoryRef](../types.md#repositoryref) | **Required** | Unwrapped without a check [`services_agent.rs#L4790`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4790). |
| `meta` | 2 | [RequestMeta](../types.md#requestmeta) | **Optional** | Only echoed [`services_agent.rs#L4794`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4794) [`services_agent.rs#L4811`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4811). |
| `session_id` | 3 | `string` | **Unconfirmed** | The parent session, passed unchecked to the repository's fork [`services_agent.rs#L4795`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4795) [`services_agent.rs#L4800-L4802`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4800-L4802). |
| `forked_session_id` | 4 | `string` | **Unconfirmed** | The child session ID, passed unchecked to the repository's fork [`services_agent.rs#L4796`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4796) [`services_agent.rs#L4800-L4802`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4800-L4802). |
| `at_turn` | 5 | `optional uint32` | **Optional** | The last inherited turn. Default 0 [`services_agent.rs#L4798`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4798). |

**Response**: `ForkSessionResponse` [`provenance_messages.proto#L349-L357`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L349-L357)

| Field | # | Type | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `session_id` | 1 | `string` | The request's `forked_session_id`, echoed [`services_agent.rs#L4796-L4797`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4796-L4797) [`services_agent.rs#L4807`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4807). |
| `meta` | 100 | [ResponseMeta](../types.md#responsemeta) | Set when the request had `meta` [`services_agent.rs#L4811`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4811). |
| `parent_manifest` | 2 | `optional` [Hash](../types.md#hash) | Always set to the parent manifest hash [`services_agent.rs#L4808`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4808). |
| `child_manifest` | 3 | `optional` [Hash](../types.md#hash) | Always set to the child manifest hash [`services_agent.rs#L4809`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4809). |
| `fork_turn` | 4 | `optional uint32` | Always set to the `at_turn` that was used (0 when the request had none) [`services_agent.rs#L4798`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4798) [`services_agent.rs#L4810`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4810). |

**Errors**

| gRPC status | ErrorCode | When | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `INTERNAL` | `REPOSITORY` | the repository cannot be opened, or the fork failed ([how handlers produce errors](../errors.md#how-handlers-produce-errors)) | [`services_agent.rs#L4800-L4802`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4800-L4802) [`state.rs#L100-L110`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L100-L110) |
| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `repository.authority` is not `local` | [`state.rs#L332-L340`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L332-L340) |
| `NOT_FOUND` | `REPOSITORY_NOT_FOUND` | repository not registered | [`state.rs#L341-L347`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L341-L347) |
| `INTERNAL` | none | the blocking task did not complete | [`services_agent.rs#L4805`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4805) |

**Behaviour**

- The handler does no sandbox checks. The method options exclude sandbox callers [`provenance_service.proto#L130-L131`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L130-L131).
- A retry is not recognized by `request_id` ([RequestMeta](#request-meta)). What a repeated fork with the same `forked_session_id` does is decided outside libatomic (see Open questions).

### RebuildSessionIndex

`rpc RebuildSessionIndex(RebuildSessionIndexRequest) returns (RebuildSessionIndexResponse)`. Unary [`provenance_service.proto#L134`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L134).

Backs `atomic session rebuild`. The rebuild runs in the handler, and the response carries the counts for the local report [`services_agent.rs#L4819-L4820`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4819-L4820) [`services_agent.rs#L4827-L4838`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4827-L4838).

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|
| Execution scope | `EXECUTION_SCOPE_LOCAL` [`provenance_service.proto#L135`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L135) ([method options](../types.md#method-options)) |
| Allowed caller | `CALLER_CLASS_LOCAL` [`provenance_service.proto#L137`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L137) |
| Effect | `RPC_EFFECT_REPOSITORY_WRITE` [`provenance_service.proto#L138`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L138) |
| Required capability | `provenance.write` [`provenance_service.proto#L136`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L136) |
| Implemented | yes [`services_agent.rs#L4815-L4839`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4815-L4839) |
| In-process `LocalService` | not routed [`local.rs#L358-L360`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/local.rs#L358-L360) |
| Advertised by GetCapabilities | no: absent from METHOD_MATRIX [`services.rs#L97-L109`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services.rs#L97-L109) |

**Request**: `RebuildSessionIndexRequest` [`provenance_messages.proto#L359-L362`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L359-L362)

| Field | # | Type | Required | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `repository` | 1 | [RepositoryRef](../types.md#repositoryref) | **Required** | Unwrapped without a check [`services_agent.rs#L4822`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4822). |

| Field | # | Type | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `meta` | 100 | [ResponseMeta](../types.md#responsemeta) | Set when the request had `meta` [`services_agent.rs#L4837`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4837). |

| `corrupt` | 3 | `uint64` | Corrupt entries found [`services_agent.rs#L4836`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4836). |

**Errors**

| gRPC status | ErrorCode | When | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `INTERNAL` | `REPOSITORY` | the repository cannot be opened, or the rebuild failed ([how handlers produce errors](../errors.md#how-handlers-produce-errors)) | [`services_agent.rs#L4828-L4829`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4828-L4829) [`state.rs#L100-L110`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L100-L110) |
| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `repository.authority` is not `local` | [`state.rs#L332-L340`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L332-L340) |
| `NOT_FOUND` | `REPOSITORY_NOT_FOUND` | repository not registered | [`state.rs#L341-L347`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L341-L347) |
| `INTERNAL` | none | the blocking task did not complete | [`services_agent.rs#L4832`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4832) |

**Behaviour**

- The handler has no local-caller check of its own [`services_agent.rs#L4815-L4826`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4815-L4826). The LOCAL-only caller restriction must be enforced by the host ([authentication and authorization](../transport.md#authentication-and-authorization)).

### GetProvenance

`rpc GetProvenance(GetProvenanceRequest) returns (GetProvenanceResponse)`. Unary [`provenance_service.proto#L142`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L142).

**Not implemented in libatomic at this commit.** The handler returns `UNIMPLEMENTED` "GetProvenance lands with its slice" [`services_agent.rs#L4841-L4846`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4841-L4846). In the contract it returns the captured per-turn provenance for a change [`provenance_messages.proto#L380`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L380). The view must match the sandbox grant, and the change must be visible [`provenance_messages.proto#L377`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L377).

### ExportProvenance

`rpc ExportProvenance(ExportProvenanceRequest) returns (ExportProvenanceResponse)`. Unary [`provenance_service.proto#L151`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L151).

Returns the unsigned projection data. Private-key signing stays with the caller [`provenance_service.proto#L150`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L150). The handler resolves a change target and loads the provenance graphs that explain the change, newest first. It maps each graph to the projection input and pre-walks the prior-turn chain that the human trace renders. The CLI projects and signs on the client over these bundles [`services_agent.rs#L5244-L5248`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5244-L5248).

| Allowed caller | `CALLER_CLASS_LOCAL`, `CALLER_CLASS_REMOTE_IDENTITY`, `CALLER_CLASS_SANDBOX` [`provenance_service.proto#L154-L156`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L154-L156) |

| Required capability | `provenance.read` [`provenance_service.proto#L153`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L153). | `change` | 3 | [Hash](../types.md#hash) | **Optional** | Used only when `target` is absent. A value that is not exactly 32 bytes counts as absent, and the algorithm is not checked [`services_agent.rs#L5260-L5266`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5260-L5266). With neither `target` nor a usable `change`, the handler returns `INVALID_ARGUMENT` "change target required" [`services_agent.rs#L5272-L5277`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5272-L5277). |

Default empty string [`services_agent.rs#L5267`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5267). Copied into every projection input [`services_agent.rs#L5212`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5212). |
| `target` | 7 | `optional string` | **Optional** | The raw `provenance trace/show` target: a bare base32 hash, a hash prefix, or `urn:atomic:change:<base32>` [`provenance_messages.proto#L392-L397`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L392-L397). When set it takes precedence over `change` [`services_agent.rs#L5272-L5273`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5272-L5273). Resolution rules are under Behaviour. |

**Response**: `ExportProvenanceResponse` [`provenance_messages.proto#L399-L409`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L399-L409). Field 2 is reserved: the caller signs, and the daemon performs no private-key operation under a read capability [`provenance_messages.proto#L401`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L401).

The handler always returns it empty [`services_agent.rs#L5352`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5352). |

| `graphs` | 4 | `repeated` [ProvenanceTraceBundle](#provenancetracebundle) | One bundle per explaining graph, newest first by graph timestamp [`services_agent.rs#L5298-L5302`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5298-L5302). Never empty on success [`services_agent.rs#L5289-L5297`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5289-L5297). |

| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | invalid base32 after `urn:atomic:change:` | [`services_agent.rs#L5366-L5373`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5366-L5373) |

| `NOT_FOUND` | `NOT_FOUND` | "change not found": no prefix match, and the target is not a full base32 hash | [`services_agent.rs#L5382-L5385`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5382-L5385) |

| `INTERNAL` | `REPOSITORY` | the graph lookup failed ([how handlers produce errors](../errors.md#how-handlers-produce-errors)) | [`services_agent.rs#L5281-L5288`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5281-L5288) |
| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | "no provenance graph explains change ..." | [`services_agent.rs#L5289-L5297`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5289-L5297) |
| `INTERNAL` | none | a graph could not be JSON-encoded | [`services_agent.rs#L5331-L5332`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5331-L5332) |
| `INTERNAL` | `REPOSITORY` | the repository cannot be opened read-only | [`services_agent.rs#L5269`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5269) [`state.rs#L219-L229`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L219-L229) |
| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `repository.authority` is not `local` | [`state.rs#L332-L340`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L332-L340) |
| `NOT_FOUND` | `REPOSITORY_NOT_FOUND` | repository not registered | [`state.rs#L341-L347`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L341-L347) |
| `INTERNAL` | none | the blocking task did not complete | [`services_agent.rs#L5350`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5350) |

**Behaviour**

- **Target resolution.** For a target with the prefix `urn:atomic:change:`, the remainder is decoded as base32. Any other target is prefix-matched against every change in the store. Exactly one match is used, and several matches are refused as ambiguous. With no match, the target itself is decoded as a full base32 hash, and `NOT_FOUND` is returned if that fails [`services_agent.rs#L5358-L5399`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5358-L5399). The prefix test is `hash.to_base32().starts_with(target)`, with the target neither trimmed nor upper-cased [`services_agent.rs#L5374-L5381`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5374-L5381). GetChange trims and upper-cases `ChangeRef.prefix` before matching [`services_query.rs#L369`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_query.rs#L369), and ListAttestations upper-cases its prefix [`services_agent.rs#L3592-L3598`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L3592-L3598), so a lowercase or space-padded prefix that those methods resolve matches nothing here and falls through to the full-hash decode. A set but empty `target` matches every change: with one change in the store it resolves to that change, and with more it is refused as ambiguous [`services_agent.rs#L5382-L5397`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5382-L5397).
- **Graph lookup.** The indexed lookup runs first. If it finds nothing, the handler falls back to a scan [`services_agent.rs#L5278-L5288`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5278-L5288).
- **Prior-turn chain.** For each graph the handler follows `previous` for at most 64 hops. A graph that cannot be loaded ends the chain with an empty-string entry, and hitting the cap sets `chain_truncated` [`services_agent.rs#L5304-L5330`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5304-L5330).
- The handler uses a read-only repository handle [`services_agent.rs#L5269`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5269).
- The handler does no sandbox checks, and it ignores `view`. ExportProvenance is on the sandbox allowlist [`sandbox_grants.rs#L88`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/sandbox_grants.rs#L88) but not on the grant-scoped list [`sandbox_grants.rs#L105-L127`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/sandbox_grants.rs#L105-L127).

### DispatchTurnEvent

`rpc DispatchTurnEvent(DispatchTurnEventRequest) returns (DispatchTurnEventResponse)`. Unary [`provenance_service.proto#L167`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L167).

A transitional seam for typed turn dispatch. The Reactor bridge delivers verified TurnEvents here, and the daemon hosts the turn orchestrator (session view, recording, journal checkpoint) in-process while the client-side decomposition lands [`provenance_service.proto#L160-L166`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L160-L166). The handler builds a TurnEvent from the body and runs a `TurnOrchestrator` whose journal sink writes directly to the repository's store. It returns the recorded change, view, files and warnings [`services_agent.rs#L4556-L4598`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4556-L4598). Every dispatch outcome, success or failure, is also recorded in the hook log [`services_agent.rs#L4601-L4625`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4601-L4625).

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|

| Field | # | Type | Required | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `repository` | 1 | [RepositoryRef](../types.md#repositoryref) | **Required** | Unwrapped without a check [`services_agent.rs#L4502`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4502). |

| `agent_id` | 3 | `string` | **Unconfirmed** | Agent registry key, for example "opencode" [`provenance_messages.proto#L429`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L429). Passed unchecked to the orchestrator and to the hook-log entry [`services_agent.rs#L4507`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4507) [`services_agent.rs#L4580`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4580) [`services_agent.rs#L4601-L4604`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4601-L4604). |
| `agent_display_name` | 4 | `optional string` | **Optional** | Defaults to `agent_id` [`services_agent.rs#L4508-L4511`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4508-L4511). |
| `agent_identity` | 5 | `optional string` | **Optional** | Resolved on the client [`provenance_messages.proto#L431`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L431), and passed to the orchestrator as is [`services_agent.rs#L4512`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4512) [`services_agent.rs#L4581`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4581). |
| `event` | 6 | `TurnEventBody` | **Required** | If it is absent the handler returns `INVALID_ARGUMENT` "typed event required" [`services_agent.rs#L4542-L4544`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4542-L4544). Its fields are below. |

`TurnEventBody` [`provenance_messages.proto#L434-L443`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L434-L443):

| Field | # | Type | Required | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session_id` | 1 | `string` | **Unconfirmed** | Passed unchecked into the TurnEvent [`services_agent.rs#L4556-L4557`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4556-L4557). |
| `event_type` | 2 | `string` | **Required** | One of `session_start`, `session_end`, `turn_start`, `turn_end`, `pre_tool_use`, `post_tool_use` [`convert.rs#L117-L128`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/convert.rs#L117-L128). Any other value gives `INVALID_ARGUMENT` "unknown event type" [`services_agent.rs#L4545-L4550`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4545-L4550). |
| `prompt` | 3 | `optional string` | **Optional** | Copied into the event [`services_agent.rs#L4560`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4560). |
| `tool_name` | 4 | `optional string` | **Optional** | Copied into the event [`services_agent.rs#L4561`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4561). |
| `tool_use_id` | 5 | `optional string` | **Optional** | Copied into the event [`services_agent.rs#L4562`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4562). |
| `timestamp` | 6 | `google.protobuf.Timestamp` | **Optional** | When absent, the server clock is used [`services_agent.rs#L4563-L4567`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4563-L4567). An out-of-range value also falls back to the server clock [`convert.rs#L28-L31`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/convert.rs#L28-L31). |
| `raw_json` | 7 | `optional bytes` | **Optional** | Opaque agent context. The proto says it is never parsed [`provenance_messages.proto#L441`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L441), but the handler parses it as JSON, and empty or invalid JSON is silently dropped [`services_agent.rs#L4551-L4555`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4551-L4555). |
| `parent_session_id` | 8 | `optional string` | **Optional** | Used only for the hook-log entry's parent session, and only when nonempty [`services_agent.rs#L4533-L4539`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4533-L4539). It is not passed to the orchestrator [`services_agent.rs#L4556-L4569`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4556-L4569). |

**Response**: `DispatchTurnEventResponse` [`provenance_messages.proto#L444-L452`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L444-L452)

| Field | # | Type | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|

| `change_hash` | 3 | `optional` [Hash](../types.md#hash) | The recorded change's hash, when one was recorded [`services_agent.rs#L4591`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4591). |

| `files` | 5 | `repeated string` | The recorded change's file list. Empty when nothing was recorded [`services_agent.rs#L4593-L4595`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4593-L4595). |
| `warnings` | 6 | `repeated string` | Warnings from the dispatch [`services_agent.rs#L4596`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4596). |
| `meta` | 100 | [ResponseMeta](../types.md#responsemeta) | Set when the request had `meta` [`services_agent.rs#L4597`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4597). |

**Errors**

| gRPC status | ErrorCode | When | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | "typed event required" | [`services_agent.rs#L4542-L4544`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4542-L4544) |
| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | "unknown event type '...'" | [`services_agent.rs#L4545-L4550`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4545-L4550) [`hook_health_dispatch.rs#L169-L173`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/tests/hook_health_dispatch.rs#L169-L173) |
| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `PROVENANCE_STORE` | the journal sink cannot resolve the repository database | [`services_agent.rs#L4572-L4573`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4572-L4573) [`journal_sink.rs#L33-L40`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/journal_sink.rs#L33-L40) |
| `INTERNAL` | `INTERNAL` | "orchestrator: ...", the orchestrator could not be created | [`services_agent.rs#L4574-L4579`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4574-L4579) [`hook_health_dispatch.rs#L187-L196`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/tests/hook_health_dispatch.rs#L187-L196) |
| `INTERNAL` | `INTERNAL` | "dispatch failed: ..." | [`services_agent.rs#L4583-L4585`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4583-L4585) |
| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `repository.authority` is not `local` | [`state.rs#L332-L340`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L332-L340) |
| `NOT_FOUND` | `REPOSITORY_NOT_FOUND` | repository not registered | [`state.rs#L341-L347`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L341-L347) |

**Behaviour**

- **Hook-log record.** Each call writes one hook-log entry whose verb is `event_type`, or `dispatch_turn_event` when `event` is absent [`services_agent.rs#L4513-L4517`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4513-L4517). The entry is appended to the store's session log. If that storage is unavailable, it is spooled to disk instead [`services_agent.rs#L4607-L4624`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4607-L4624). The tests show that a rejected event is still recorded with outcome "error" and the error detail [`hook_health_dispatch.rs#L169-L181`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/tests/hook_health_dispatch.rs#L169-L181). A later success records outcome "ok" with no detail [`hook_health_dispatch.rs#L198-L207`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/tests/hook_health_dispatch.rs#L198-L207).
- Concurrent callers are serialized by the repository gate [`services_agent.rs#L4504-L4505`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4504-L4505). The test fires 24 overlapping invalid events and finds 24 hook-log entries afterwards [`hook_health_dispatch.rs#L209-L229`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/tests/hook_health_dispatch.rs#L209-L229).
- The in-process journal sink opens the store for each operation and holds no handle [`journal_sink.rs#L23-L31`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/journal_sink.rs#L23-L31). It reads the frozen journal in one unpaged call [`journal_sink.rs#L222-L233`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/journal_sink.rs#L222-L233).
- The server-clock fallback for `timestamp` differs from the contract's rule that observation times are supplied by the caller [`common.proto#L18-L22`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/common/common.proto#L18-L22).

### ExplainTurns

`rpc ExplainTurns(ExplainTurnsRequest) returns (ExplainTurnsResponse)`. Unary [`provenance_service.proto#L185`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L185).

Backs `atomic agent explain`. The handler loads the turns recorded on a view and, for each selected turn, extracts the condensed transcript, generates the reasoning with the Claude CLI and anchors code learnings to the graph. With `save` it also writes the reasoning back into the change and appends learnings to the agent context file [`provenance_service.proto#L176-L184`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L176-L184) [`services_agent.rs#L4917-L4919`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4917-L4919).

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|
| Execution scope | `EXECUTION_SCOPE_LOCAL` [`provenance_service.proto#L186`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L186) ([method options](../types.md#method-options)) |
| Allowed caller | `CALLER_CLASS_LOCAL` [`provenance_service.proto#L190`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L190) |
| Effect | `RPC_EFFECT_REPOSITORY_READ`, `RPC_EFFECT_REPOSITORY_WRITE`, `RPC_EFFECT_WORKSPACE_WRITE`, `RPC_EFFECT_EXTERNAL_IO` (the Claude CLI subprocess) [`provenance_service.proto#L191-L194`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L191-L194) |
| Required capability | `repository.read`, `repository.write`, `working-copy.write` [`provenance_service.proto#L187-L189`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L187-L189) |
| Implemented | yes [`services_agent.rs#L4855-L4860`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4855-L4860) [`services_agent.rs#L4920-L5165`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4920-L5165) |
| In-process `LocalService` | not routed [`local.rs#L358-L360`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/local.rs#L358-L360) |
| Advertised by GetCapabilities | no: absent from METHOD_MATRIX [`services.rs#L97-L109`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services.rs#L97-L109) |

**Request**: `ExplainTurnsRequest` [`provenance_messages.proto#L456-L470`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L456-L470)

| Field | # | Type | Required | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `repository` | 1 | [RepositoryRef](../types.md#repositoryref) | **Required** | Unwrapped without a check [`services_agent.rs#L4925`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4925). |
| `meta` | 2 | [RequestMeta](../types.md#requestmeta) | **Optional** | Only echoed [`services_agent.rs#L4929`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4929) [`services_agent.rs#L5163`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5163). |
| `session_id` | 3 | `string` | **Ignored** | Copied and then discarded [`services_agent.rs#L4930`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4930) [`services_agent.rs#L5158`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5158). The turns come from the history of `view_name` [`services_agent.rs#L4946-L4947`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4946-L4947). |
| `turn` | 4 | `optional uint32` | **Optional** | 1-indexed. Absent means the most recent entry. 0 gives "Turn number must be >= 1", and a number past the end gives "Turn N not found" [`services_agent.rs#L4958-L4978`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4958-L4978). |
| `all` | 5 | `bool` | **Optional** | Default `false`. When `true`, every entry is explained and `turn` is not read [`services_agent.rs#L4958-L4959`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4958-L4959). |
| `save` | 6 | `bool` | **Optional** | Default `false`. When `true`, the reasoning is saved into the change and learnings into the context file [`services_agent.rs#L5096-L5135`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5096-L5135). |
| `model` | 7 | `string` | **Unconfirmed** | The Claude CLI model, passed to the generator unchanged [`services_agent.rs#L5034`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5034). |
| `view_name` | 8 | `string` | **Unconfirmed** | The view whose history supplies the turns. A view that does not exist gives a `VIEW` error [`services_agent.rs#L4946-L4953`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4946-L4953). |
| `transcript_path` | 9 | `optional string` | **Optional** | A transcript file that the daemon reads from its own filesystem when the change has no condensed transcript [`services_agent.rs#L4891-L4892`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4891-L4892). |
| `files_touched` | 10 | `repeated string` | **Optional** | The last-resort file list for a turn [`services_agent.rs#L4902-L4910`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4902-L4910) [`services_agent.rs#L5016-L5032`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5016-L5032). |
| `agent_name` | 11 | `string` | **Optional** | A name containing "gemini" selects the JSON transcript format; anything else selects JSONL [`services_agent.rs#L4893-L4897`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4893-L4897). It also selects the agent context file for learnings [`services_agent.rs#L5121-L5125`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5121-L5125). |

**Response**: `ExplainTurnsResponse` [`provenance_messages.proto#L471-L476`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L471-L476)

| Field | # | Type | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `turns` | 1 | `repeated` [ExplainTurnResult](#explainturnresult) | One result per selected turn, in history order [`services_agent.rs#L4980-L4981`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4980-L4981) [`services_agent.rs#L5160-L5161`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5160-L5161). |
| `session_has_no_turns` | 2 | `bool` | `true` when the view's history is empty. `turns` is then empty [`services_agent.rs#L4954-L4956`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4954-L4956). |
| `meta` | 100 | [ResponseMeta](../types.md#responsemeta) | Set when the request had `meta` [`services_agent.rs#L5163`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5163). |

**Errors**

| gRPC status | ErrorCode | When | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `PERMISSION_DENIED` | `VIEW` | "view '...' does not exist" | [`services_agent.rs#L4948-L4951`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4948-L4951) [`state.rs#L442`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L442) |
| `INTERNAL` | `REPOSITORY` | any other history-read failure, or the repository cannot be opened ([how handlers produce errors](../errors.md#how-handlers-produce-errors)) | [`services_agent.rs#L4952`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4952) [`services_agent.rs#L4945`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4945) |
| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `turn` is 0 | [`services_agent.rs#L4961-L4963`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4961-L4963) |
| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `turn` is past the end of the history | [`services_agent.rs#L4964-L4974`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4964-L4974) |
| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `CHANGES` | "turn index out of range", or a turn's change could not be loaded | [`services_agent.rs#L4982-L4990`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4982-L4990) |
| `INTERNAL` | none | the reasoning could not be JSON-encoded | [`services_agent.rs#L5091-L5092`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5091-L5092) |
| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `repository.authority` is not `local` | [`state.rs#L332-L340`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L332-L340) |
| `NOT_FOUND` | `REPOSITORY_NOT_FOUND` | repository not registered | [`state.rs#L341-L347`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L341-L347) |
| `INTERNAL` | none | the blocking task did not complete | [`services_agent.rs#L5157`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5157) |

**Behaviour**

- Failures inside a single turn do not fail the RPC; they are reported in that turn's result ([ExplainTurnResult](#explainturnresult)) [`services_agent.rs#L4999-L5013`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4999-L5013) [`services_agent.rs#L5036-L5053`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5036-L5053) [`services_agent.rs#L5110-L5131`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5110-L5131).
- `turn_number` is the 1-based position in the view's history [`services_agent.rs#L4992`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4992), not a session-ledger turn number.
- The handler holds the repository gate for the whole call [`services_agent.rs#L4927-L4928`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4927-L4928) [`services_agent.rs#L4944`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4944), which runs one Claude CLI generation per selected turn [`services_agent.rs#L5034-L5036`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5034-L5036).
- The handler has no local-caller check of its own [`services_agent.rs#L4920-L4943`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4920-L4943). It reads a daemon-side path from the request [`services_agent.rs#L4891-L4892`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4891-L4892) and writes the change and the context file [`services_agent.rs#L5112`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5112) [`services_agent.rs#L5121-L5125`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5121-L5125). The LOCAL-only caller restriction must be enforced by the host ([authentication and authorization](../transport.md#authentication-and-authorization)).

### ListSessions

`rpc ListSessions(ListSessionsRequest) returns (ListSessionsResponse)`. Unary [`provenance_service.proto#L197`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L197).

Returns recent session ledgers, newest activity first, for `atomic session show` with no ID [`provenance_service.proto#L174-L175`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L174-L175). The handler returns per-session summaries, the complete ledgers as versioned bytes and per-session intent counts [`services_agent.rs#L4705-L4779`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4705-L4779).

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|
| Execution scope | `EXECUTION_SCOPE_BOTH` [`provenance_service.proto#L198`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L198) ([method options](../types.md#method-options)) |
| Allowed caller | `CALLER_CLASS_LOCAL`, `CALLER_CLASS_REMOTE_IDENTITY` [`provenance_service.proto#L200-L201`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L200-L201) |
| Effect | `RPC_EFFECT_REPOSITORY_READ` [`provenance_service.proto#L202`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L202) |
| Required capability | `provenance.read` [`provenance_service.proto#L199`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L199) |
| Implemented | yes [`services_agent.rs#L4691-L4780`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4691-L4780) |
| In-process `LocalService` | not routed [`local.rs#L358-L360`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/local.rs#L358-L360) |
| Advertised by GetCapabilities | yes [`services.rs#L109`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services.rs#L109), with an empty descriptor [`services.rs#L204-L212`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services.rs#L204-L212) |

**Request**: `ListSessionsRequest` [`provenance_messages.proto#L497-L500`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L497-L500). It has no `meta` field.

| Field | # | Type | Required | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `repository` | 1 | [RepositoryRef](../types.md#repositoryref) | **Required** | Unwrapped without a check [`services_agent.rs#L4696`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4696). |
| `limit` | 2 | `uint32` | **Optional** | The most recent N sessions. 0 means 10 [`services_agent.rs#L4698-L4702`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4698-L4702) [`provenance_messages.proto#L499`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L499). |

**Response**: `ListSessionsResponse` [`provenance_messages.proto#L501-L509`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L501-L509)

| Field | # | Type | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `sessions` | 1 | `repeated SessionSummary` | One summary per ledger, in the order the repository returned them [`services_agent.rs#L4738-L4769`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4738-L4769). Fields below. |
| `ledgers_bundle` | 2 | `optional` [VersionedBytes](../types.md#versionedbytes) | Schema `atomic.session.ledgers.v1`, a JSON array of `{record, turns}` pairs. Absent only if encoding fails [`services_agent.rs#L4728-L4736`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4728-L4736). |
| `intent_counts` | 3 | `repeated uint32` | Parallel to `sessions`. The number of distinct `plan_id`s in the session's turns, or when there are none, the number of vault intents linked to the session [`services_agent.rs#L4737-L4753`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4737-L4753) [`services_agent.rs#L4709-L4727`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4709-L4727). |

`SessionSummary` [`provenance_messages.proto#L510-L516`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L510-L516):

| Field | # | Type | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `session_id` | 1 | `string` | The ledger record's session ID [`services_agent.rs#L4762`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4762). |
| `view` | 2 | `optional string` | The record's view name [`provenance_messages.proto#L512`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L512) [`services_agent.rs#L4763`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4763). |
| `turn_count` | 3 | `uint32` | The larger of the record's turn count and the number of turns loaded [`services_agent.rs#L4764`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4764). |
| `started_at` | 4 | `optional google.protobuf.Timestamp` | The record's start time, when it converts [`services_agent.rs#L4754-L4756`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4754-L4756). |
| `ended_at` | 5 | `optional google.protobuf.Timestamp` | The record's end time, when set [`services_agent.rs#L4757-L4760`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4757-L4760). |

**Errors**

| gRPC status | ErrorCode | When | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `INTERNAL` | `REPOSITORY` | the repository cannot be opened, or the listing failed ([how handlers produce errors](../errors.md#how-handlers-produce-errors)) | [`services_agent.rs#L4707-L4708`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4707-L4708) [`state.rs#L100-L110`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L100-L110) |
| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | `repository.authority` is not `local` | [`state.rs#L332-L340`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L332-L340) |
| `NOT_FOUND` | `REPOSITORY_NOT_FOUND` | repository not registered | [`state.rs#L341-L347`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L341-L347) |
| `INTERNAL` | none | the blocking task did not complete | [`services_agent.rs#L4773`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4773) |

**Behaviour**

- A failure to read the vault manifest is ignored. Intent counts then come only from the turns' `plan_id`s [`services_agent.rs#L4713`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4713) [`services_agent.rs#L4743-L4752`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4743-L4752).
- The handler opens the repository with the writable open, not the read-only one [`services_agent.rs#L4707`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4707) [`state.rs#L100-L110`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L100-L110) [`state.rs#L219-L229`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L219-L229), although the method declares only `RPC_EFFECT_REPOSITORY_READ` [`provenance_service.proto#L197-L203`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L197-L203). GetSession does the same; see [Open questions](#open-questions).
- The response has no `meta` [`services_agent.rs#L4775-L4779`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4775-L4779).

## Types

### HookLogEvent

An operational diagnostic event, used by [AppendHookLog](#appendhooklog) and [LoadHookLog](#loadhooklog) [`provenance_messages.proto#L58-L69`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L58-L69). The handlers copy every field unchanged between the wire and the store [`services_provenance.rs#L44-L74`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L44-L74).

| Field | # | Type | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `event_id` | 1 | `string` | Event identifier. The test uses a UUID v4 [`hook_health_dispatch.rs#L249`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/tests/hook_health_dispatch.rs#L249). |
| `session_id` | 2 | `optional string` | The session the event belongs to. LoadHookLog filters on it [`services_provenance.rs#L128-L129`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L128-L129). |
| `parent_session_id` | 3 | `optional string` | The parent session. DispatchTurnEvent fills it from the event body [`services_agent.rs#L4533-L4539`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4533-L4539), and the test checks that it is stored [`hook_health_dispatch.rs#L237-L243`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/tests/hook_health_dispatch.rs#L237-L243). |
| `agent` | 4 | `string` | The agent key, for example "opencode" [`hook_health_dispatch.rs#L252`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/tests/hook_health_dispatch.rs#L252). |
| `display_name` | 5 | `string` | The agent's display name [`hook_health_dispatch.rs#L253`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/tests/hook_health_dispatch.rs#L253). |
| `verb` | 6 | `string` | The hook verb. DispatchTurnEvent uses the event type [`services_agent.rs#L4513-L4517`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4513-L4517). |
| `outcome` | 7 | `string` | `ok` or `error` [`provenance_messages.proto#L66`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L66). |
| `detail` | 8 | `optional string` | Error detail. The test checks that it carries the error text [`hook_health_dispatch.rs#L177-L181`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/tests/hook_health_dispatch.rs#L177-L181). |
| `at` | 9 | `string` | RFC 3339 time, kept unchanged across retries [`provenance_messages.proto#L68`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L68). |

### StoredProvenanceTurn

A provenance turn, returned by [ReserveTurn](#reserveturn), [UpdateTurn](#updateturn), [GetTurn](#getturn) and [GetSession](#getsession) [`provenance_messages.proto#L128-L142`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L128-L142). The journal RPCs fill it from the stored turn [`services_provenance.rs#L169-L193`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L169-L193). GetSession fills only a few fields ([GetSession](#getsession)).

| Field | # | Type | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `schema_version` | 1 | `uint32` | The stored record's schema version [`services_provenance.rs#L179`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L179). |
| `provenance_id` | 2 | `uint64` | The stable turn ID (`ProvenanceId(u64)`) [`provenance_messages.proto#L130`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L130). Pass it to AppendEnvelopes, PrepareCheckpoint, LoadFrozenEnvelopes, BindCheckpointHash and AcknowledgeCheckpoint [`provenance_messages.proto#L220`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L220) [`provenance_messages.proto#L232`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L232) [`provenance_messages.proto#L244`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L244) [`provenance_messages.proto#L258`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L258) [`provenance_messages.proto#L271`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L271). |
| `session_id` | 3 | `string` | The session [`services_provenance.rs#L181`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L181). |
| `turn_number` | 4 | `uint32` | The turn number within the session [`services_provenance.rs#L182`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L182). |
| `state` | 5 | [ProvenanceTurnState](#provenanceturnstate) | The lifecycle state [`services_provenance.rs#L171-L177`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L171-L177) [`services_provenance.rs#L183`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L183). |
| `generation` | 6 | `uint64` | The granted generation used for fencing [`provenance_messages.proto#L134`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L134) [`services_provenance.rs#L184`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L184). |
| `next_event_seq` | 7 | `uint64` | The next journal sequence [`services_provenance.rs#L185`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L185). |
| `created_at` | 8 | `google.protobuf.Timestamp` | Creation time in whole seconds [`services_provenance.rs#L186`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L186) [`services_provenance.rs#L165-L167`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L165-L167). |
