| `updated_at` | 9 | `google.protobuf.Timestamp` | Last update time in whole seconds [`services_provenance.rs#L187`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L187). |
| `completed_at` | 10 | `optional google.protobuf.Timestamp` | Completion time, when the turn has completed [`services_provenance.rs#L188`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L188). |
| `final_hash` | 11 | `optional` [Hash](../types.md#hash) | The turn's final provenance hash, when bound [`services_provenance.rs#L189`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L189). |
| `checkpoint_attempt` | 12 | `optional` [ProvenanceCheckpointAttempt](#provenancecheckpointattempt) | The current checkpoint attempt, when one exists [`services_provenance.rs#L190`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L190). |
| `stop_state` | 13 | `optional` [StopState](#stopstate) | Present when the state is STOPPED or ABANDONED [`provenance_messages.proto#L141`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L141) [`services_provenance.rs#L171-L177`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L171-L177). |

### ProvenanceTurnState

`enum ProvenanceTurnState` [`provenance_messages.proto#L105-L112`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L105-L112). The journal RPCs map the five stored states one-to-one [`services_provenance.rs#L171-L177`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L171-L177). GetSession always returns UNSPECIFIED [`services_query.rs#L704`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_query.rs#L704).

| Value | # |
|---|---|
| `PROVENANCE_TURN_STATE_UNSPECIFIED` | 0 |
| `PROVENANCE_TURN_STATE_RUNNING` | 1 |
| `PROVENANCE_TURN_STATE_STOPPED` | 2 |
| `PROVENANCE_TURN_STATE_CHECKPOINTING` | 3 |
| `PROVENANCE_TURN_STATE_COMPLETED` | 4 |
| `PROVENANCE_TURN_STATE_ABANDONED` | 5 |

### StopState

Why and when a turn stopped [`provenance_messages.proto#L121-L126`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L121-L126). The handler maps it from the store [`services_provenance.rs#L195-L209`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L195-L209).

| Field | # | Type | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `cause` | 1 | [StopCause](#stopcause) | The stop cause [`services_provenance.rs#L197-L204`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L197-L204). |
| `observed_at` | 2 | `google.protobuf.Timestamp` | The caller-supplied observation time of the stop, in whole seconds [`services_provenance.rs#L205`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L205). |
| `last_event_seq` | 3 | `optional uint64` | The last journal sequence at the stop [`services_provenance.rs#L206`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L206). |
| `resumable` | 4 | `bool` | Whether RESUME may continue the turn [`services_provenance.rs#L207`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L207). ABANDON always sets it to `false` [`provenance_core.rs#L419-L425`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L419-L425). |

### StopCause

`enum StopCause` [`provenance_messages.proto#L95-L103`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L95-L103). It is used in [StopState](#stopstate) and in UpdateTurn's `cause`. UpdateTurn rejects `STOP_CAUSE_UNSPECIFIED` for STOP [`services_provenance.rs#L861-L868`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L861-L868).

| Value | # |
|---|---|
| `STOP_CAUSE_UNSPECIFIED` | 0 |
| `STOP_CAUSE_USER_REQUESTED` | 1 |
| `STOP_CAUSE_LEASE_EXPIRED` | 2 |
| `STOP_CAUSE_PROCESS_EXITED` | 3 |
| `STOP_CAUSE_HOOK_FAILURE` | 4 |
| `STOP_CAUSE_SYSTEM_SHUTDOWN` | 5 |
| `STOP_CAUSE_ABANDONED` | 6 |

### ProvenanceCheckpointAttempt

One recoverable checkpoint attempt [`provenance_messages.proto#L155-L165`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L155-L165), returned by [PrepareCheckpoint](#preparecheckpoint) and [BindCheckpointHash](#bindcheckpointhash) and carried in [StoredProvenanceTurn](#storedprovenanceturn). The handler maps it from the store [`services_provenance.rs#L223-L247`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L223-L247).

| Field | # | Type | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `attempt_generation` | 1 | `uint64` | The proto says bind and acknowledge advance to this generation [`provenance_messages.proto#L156`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L156). LoadFrozenEnvelopes takes it [`provenance_messages.proto#L245`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L245), and the in-process sink sends it as the expected generation for bind and acknowledge [`journal_sink.rs#L242-L249`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/journal_sink.rs#L242-L249) [`journal_sink.rs#L264-L269`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/journal_sink.rs#L264-L269). |
| `frozen_event_count` | 2 | `uint64` | The exclusive journal-sequence cutoff of the frozen journal [`provenance_messages.proto#L157`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L157) [`services_provenance.rs#L232`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L232). |
| `source` | 3 | [CheckpointSource](#checkpointsource) | The frozen source. Always set [`services_provenance.rs#L233`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L233). |
| `phase` | 4 | [CheckpointPhase](#checkpointphase) | The attempt's phase [`services_provenance.rs#L225-L229`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L225-L229) [`services_provenance.rs#L234`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L234). |
| `provenance_hash` | 5 | `optional` [Hash](../types.md#hash) | The hash bound to the attempt, when set [`services_provenance.rs#L235`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L235). |
| `session_turn` | 6 | `optional` [SessionTurn](../types.md#sessionturn) | The bound session turn's `(session_id, turn_number)`, when set [`services_provenance.rs#L236-L242`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L236-L242). |
| `manifest_hash` | 7 | `optional` [Hash](../types.md#hash) | The manifest hash, when set [`services_provenance.rs#L243`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L243). |
| `prepared_at` | 8 | `google.protobuf.Timestamp` | Prepare time, in whole seconds. It is also the session turn's timestamp at bind [`services_provenance.rs#L244`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L244) [`services_provenance.rs#L313-L315`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L313-L315). |
| `updated_at` | 9 | `google.protobuf.Timestamp` | Last update time, in whole seconds [`services_provenance.rs#L245`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L245). |

### CheckpointSource

The checkpoint's source, with the same fields as the store's `ProvenanceCheckpointSource` [`provenance_messages.proto#L144-L153`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L144-L153). It is sent in PrepareCheckpoint and returned in [ProvenanceCheckpointAttempt](#provenancecheckpointattempt). The handlers convert it field for field in both directions [`services_provenance.rs#L211-L221`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L211-L221) [`services_provenance.rs#L260-L278`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L260-L278).

| Field | # | Type | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `agent_name` | 1 | `string` | Agent name [`services_provenance.rs#L262`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L262). |
| `agent_display_name` | 2 | `string` | Agent display name [`services_provenance.rs#L263`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L263). |
| `agent_vendor` | 3 | `string` | Agent vendor [`services_provenance.rs#L264`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L264). |
| `change_hashes` | 4 | `repeated` [Hash](../types.md#hash) | The changes the turn explains. On input, entries that are not exactly 32 bytes are silently dropped [`services_provenance.rs#L265-L270`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L265-L270). For a sandbox, every entry must be on its view [`services_provenance.rs#L389-L406`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L389-L406). |
| `previous_provenance` | 5 | `optional` [Hash](../types.md#hash) | The previous provenance hash. On input, a value that is not exactly 32 bytes is dropped [`services_provenance.rs#L271-L274`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L271-L274). |
| `plan_id` | 6 | `optional string` | Plan ID [`services_provenance.rs#L275`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L275). |
| `ledger_turn_number` | 7 | `uint32` | The turn's position in the session ledger [`services_provenance.rs#L276`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L276). |

### CheckpointPhase

`enum CheckpointPhase` [`provenance_messages.proto#L114-L119`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L114-L119). The handler maps the store's three phases one-to-one [`services_provenance.rs#L225-L229`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L225-L229).

| Value | # |
|---|---|
| `CHECKPOINT_PHASE_UNSPECIFIED` | 0 |
| `CHECKPOINT_PHASE_PREPARED` | 1 |
| `CHECKPOINT_PHASE_HASH_BOUND` | 2 |
| `CHECKPOINT_PHASE_PUBLISHED` | 3 |

### FrozenPage

One page of the frozen journal, returned by [LoadFrozenEnvelopes](#loadfrozenenvelopes) [`provenance_messages.proto#L193-L196`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L193-L196). The handler maps it from the store's page [`services_provenance.rs#L657-L677`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L657-L677).

| Field | # | Type | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `fragments` | 1 | `repeated FrozenFragment` | At most 256 fragments [`provenance_core.rs#L450-L453`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L450-L453). Each is `{cursor = 1 (`[PageCursor](../types.md#pagecursor)`), bytes = 2 (bytes), complete = 3 (bool)}`. `cursor` is `{seq, offset}`: `seq` indexes the journal envelope and `offset` the byte position inside it [`pagination.proto#L5-L10`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/common/pagination.proto#L5-L10). `complete` marks the end of an envelope [`provenance_messages.proto#L187-L191`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L187-L191). `cursor` is always set [`services_provenance.rs#L660-L667`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L660-L667). |
| `next` | 2 | [PageCursor](../types.md#pagecursor) | The cursor for the next request. The proto says an absent field means the end [`provenance_messages.proto#L195`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L195), but the handler always sets it [`services_provenance.rs#L672-L675`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L672-L675). The end is `{seq: frozen_event_count, offset: 0}` ([LoadFrozenEnvelopes](#loadfrozenenvelopes)). |

### ProvenanceTraceBundle

One explaining provenance graph, returned by [ExportProvenance](#exportprovenance) [`provenance_messages.proto#L411-L422`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L411-L422). The handler fills every field [`services_agent.rs#L5333-L5345`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5333-L5345).

| Field | # | Type | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `graph_hash` | 1 | `optional` [Hash](../types.md#hash) | The stored graph's content hash. Always set [`services_agent.rs#L5334`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5334). |
| `graph` | 2 | [VersionedBytes](../types.md#versionedbytes) | Schema `atomic.prov.graph.v1`, the domain ProvenanceGraph JSON [`services_agent.rs#L5331-L5338`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5331-L5338). |
| `input` | 3 | [VersionedBytes](../types.md#versionedbytes) | Schema `atomic.prov.input.v1`, the ProvActivityInput JSON [`services_agent.rs#L5339-L5342`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5339-L5342). Its keys are listed in [`services_agent.rs#L5224-L5242`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5224-L5242). |
| `prior_activities` | 4 | `repeated string` | Activity URNs along the `previous` chain. An empty entry means "(graph unavailable)" [`provenance_messages.proto#L418-L420`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L418-L420). The proto says oldest first [`provenance_messages.proto#L418-L420`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L418-L420), but the handler starts at the graph's immediate `previous` and walks back, so the first entry is the most recent prior turn [`services_agent.rs#L5308-L5330`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5308-L5330). |
| `chain_truncated` | 5 | `bool` | Set when the 64-hop walk cap was hit [`provenance_messages.proto#L421`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L421) [`services_agent.rs#L5313-L5316`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5313-L5316). |

### ExplainTurnResult

The outcome for one turn, returned by [ExplainTurns](#explainturns) [`provenance_messages.proto#L477-L493`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L477-L493).

| Field | # | Type | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `turn_number` | 1 | `uint32` | 1-based position in the view's history [`services_agent.rs#L4992`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4992). |
| `message` | 2 | `string` | The change's header message [`services_agent.rs#L4991`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4991). |
| `no_transcript` | 3 | `bool` | `true` when no condensed transcript was found. The turn is then skipped, with no reasoning [`services_agent.rs#L4999-L5013`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4999-L5013). |
| `reasoning` | 4 | `optional` [VersionedBytes](../types.md#versionedbytes) | Schema `atomic.agent.reasoning.v1`. Set only when generation succeeded [`services_agent.rs#L5137-L5146`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5137-L5146). |
| `generation_error` | 5 | `optional string` | The generator's error text [`services_agent.rs#L5036-L5053`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5036-L5053). It is the empty string when the generator returned empty reasoning [`services_agent.rs#L5055-L5069`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5055-L5069). |
| `anchored` | 6 | `uint32` | The number of code learnings anchored to the graph [`services_agent.rs#L5072-L5089`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5072-L5089). |
| `saved` | 7 | `bool` | `true` when `save` was requested and the change was saved [`services_agent.rs#L5109-L5115`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5109-L5115). |
| `save_error` | 8 | `optional string` | Why attaching the reasoning or saving the change failed [`services_agent.rs#L5111-L5119`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5111-L5119). |
| `context_saved` | 9 | `optional string` | The context-file save outcome, when there were learnings to save [`services_agent.rs#L5120-L5126`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5120-L5126). |
| `context_save_error` | 10 | `optional string` | Why the context-file save failed [`services_agent.rs#L5127`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5127). |
| `change` | 11 | `optional` [Hash](../types.md#hash) | The explained change's hash. Always set [`services_agent.rs#L5003`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5003) [`services_agent.rs#L5140`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5140). |

## Open questions

- **A missing `repository` panics most handlers.** Every handler except the two hook-log ones calls `request.repository.as_ref().unwrap()` [`services_provenance.rs#L430`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L430) [`services_agent.rs#L4502`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4502) [`services_query.rs#L587`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_query.rs#L587) [`services_agent.rs#L5255`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5255). The status a client receives when the handler panics depends on the host's server stack, which libatomic does not include [`sandbox_grants.rs#L9-L10`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/sandbox_grants.rs#L9-L10).
- **Replay detection and `IDEMPOTENCY_MISMATCH` are not implemented.** The contract and proto require replay detection and `IDEMPOTENCY_MISMATCH` [`CONTRACT.md#L54-L58`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/CONTRACT.md?plain=1#L54-L58) [`common.proto#L8-L17`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/common/common.proto#L8-L17) [`provenance_messages.proto#L19-L20`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L19-L20), and the README calls request-ID echo and replay detection core semantics [`README.md#L132-L135`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/README.md?plain=1#L132-L135). The handlers only echo `request_id` with `replayed = false` [`services_agent.rs#L1233-L1239`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L1233-L1239). The journal sink says request-ID echo "lives in the store layer itself" [`journal_sink.rs#L7-L9`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/journal_sink.rs#L7-L9), but no request ID is passed to the store [`services_provenance.rs#L522-L528`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L522-L528). A client cannot tell a replayed outcome from a fresh one.
- **A generation conflict has no distinct error code.** The proto and the error enum name `GENERATION_CONFLICT` for fencing mismatches [`provenance_messages.proto#L15-L18`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L15-L18) [`error.proto#L41`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/common/error.proto#L41). The handlers classify the store's fencing error as `PROVENANCE_STORE` or `PROVENANCE_CHECKPOINT`, the same as every other store failure [`provenance_core.rs#L131-L133`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L131-L133) [`provenance_core.rs#L166`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L166) [`provenance_core.rs#L331`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L331) [`provenance_core.rs#L346`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L346). Only the message text "generation is stale" identifies it [`provenance_core.rs#L11-L12`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L11-L12), and that text is produced in atomic-repository.
- **Generation arithmetic is decided outside libatomic.** The proto says bind and acknowledge "advance to attempt_generation" [`provenance_messages.proto#L15-L18`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L15-L18) [`provenance_service.proto#L74-L76`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L74-L76). libatomic only shows that bind and acknowledge are sent `attempt_generation` as their expected generation [`journal_sink.rs#L242-L249`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/journal_sink.rs#L242-L249) [`journal_sink.rs#L264-L269`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/journal_sink.rs#L264-L269). Which operation changes the turn's generation, and what `generation` holds after prepare, stop, resume, abandon and acknowledge, is decided in atomic-repository and could not be confirmed from libatomic.
- **LoadFrozenEnvelopes never omits `next`.** The proto says an absent `next` means the end [`provenance_messages.proto#L195`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L195), and the pagination primitive says servers return the next cursor "if more data remains" [`pagination.proto#L26-L28`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/common/pagination.proto#L26-L28). The handler always sets it [`services_provenance.rs#L672-L675`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L672-L675). This page documents the end condition `{frozen_event_count, 0}` from the in-process loader [`provenance_core.rs#L296`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L296). The proto has not been updated to match.
- **LoadFrozenEnvelopes with `budget.max_bytes = 0`.** The handler passes `min(0, budget) = 0` to the store [`provenance_core.rs#L206-L208`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L206-L208). The result, an error or an empty page, is decided in atomic-repository. `max_items` is never read [`services_provenance.rs#L633-L637`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L633-L637).
- **What is BindCheckpointHash's `hash`?** The proto calls it the manifest hash [`provenance_messages.proto#L260`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L260) [`provenance_service.proto#L74`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L74). The handler binds it as the session turn's `provenance_hash` [`services_provenance.rs#L298`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L298), the attempt reports `provenance_hash` and `manifest_hash` separately [`services_provenance.rs#L235`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L235) [`services_provenance.rs#L243`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L243), and AcknowledgeCheckpoint carries its own `manifest_hash` [`provenance_messages.proto#L273`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L273). Which hash a client must send at bind is not settled by the proto comments.
- **UpdateTurn's `expected_generation` is not used for fencing.** The proto and contract require it [`provenance_messages.proto#L295`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L295) [`CONTRACT.md#L216`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/CONTRACT.md?plain=1#L216). The handler checks only that it is nonzero and never compares it with the turn [`services_provenance.rs#L837-L845`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L837-L845) [`provenance_core.rs#L360-L363`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L360-L363). It is unclear whether this narrowing is intended to last.
- **An absent `observed_at` silently becomes 0.** Without `meta.observed_at`, the journal handlers record time 0 [`services_provenance.rs#L158-L163`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L158-L163), and nanoseconds are always truncated [`services_provenance.rs#L165-L167`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L165-L167). The contract says observation time is supplied by the caller [`CONTRACT.md#L52-L53`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/CONTRACT.md?plain=1#L52-L53) but does not say whether its absence must be rejected.
- **Store-side validation of identifiers.** `provenance_id`, `session_id`, `turn_number`, `expected_generation`, `attempt_generation` and `frozen_event_count` are passed to the store without checks, for example [`services_provenance.rs#L522-L528`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L522-L528) [`services_provenance.rs#L647-L656`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L647-L656) [`services_provenance.rs#L459-L461`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L459-L461). How the store treats an unknown or zero ID, an empty session ID, or a `frozen_event_count` that differs from the attempt's, and with which message, is decided in atomic-repository. `provenance_id`, `expected_generation`, and `session_id` and `turn_number` where they are lookup keys, are marked **Required**, because the calls depend on them; the rest are marked **Unconfirmed** (see [Methods](../README.md#methods)).
- **Hook-log validation and limits are outside libatomic.** AppendHookLog copies `HookLogEvent` unchecked [`services_provenance.rs#L44-L58`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L44-L58). LoadHookLog forwards any nonzero `limit` [`services_provenance.rs#L131-L135`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L131-L135), although the proto says the limit is bounded to 512 [`provenance_messages.proto#L84`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L84). Field validation, the 512 bound, and what happens when an `event_id` is reused with different contents all happen in atomic-repository.
- **Ownership survives view deletion.** The contract says deleting a view invalidates its grants and associated ownership, that recreating the same name cannot recover that authority, and that session and turn ownership checks use repository plus stable view ID, never just a name [`CONTRACT.md#L159-L164`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/CONTRACT.md?plain=1#L159-L164). libatomic's default grant store keys ownership by view name and never removes it [`sandbox_grants.rs#L309-L318`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/sandbox_grants.rs#L309-L318) [`sandbox_grants.rs#L346-L348`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/sandbox_grants.rs#L346-L348) [`sandbox_grants.rs#L442-L479`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/sandbox_grants.rs#L442-L479), so a grant for a recreated view of the same name owns the old view's sessions and turns. A host may install its own `SandboxGrants` store [`sandbox_grants.rs#L257-L268`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/sandbox_grants.rs#L257-L268); whether the default store will move to the stable view ID is not stated.
- **Sandbox scoping for the session and projection reads.** GetSession, GetProvenance and ExportProvenance accept sandbox callers in their method options [`provenance_service.proto#L122-L124`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L122-L124) [`provenance_service.proto#L154-L156`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L154-L156). They are on the sandbox allowlist [`sandbox_grants.rs#L86-L88`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/sandbox_grants.rs#L86-L88) but not on the grant-scoped list [`sandbox_grants.rs#L105-L127`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/sandbox_grants.rs#L105-L127). ExportProvenance ignores `view` [`services_agent.rs#L5254-L5267`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5254-L5267), although the proto requires GetProvenance's view to match the grant [`provenance_messages.proto#L377`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L377). Whether these handlers will enforce grants, or hosts must keep refusing sandboxes on them, is not settled.
- **PrepareCheckpoint's sandbox check is narrower than the contract.** The contract requires checks of explained changes, previous provenance and plan links [`CONTRACT.md#L169-L171`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/CONTRACT.md?plain=1#L169-L171). The handler checks only `change_hashes` [`services_provenance.rs#L575`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L575) [`services_provenance.rs#L389-L406`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_provenance.rs#L389-L406).
- **GetCapabilities descriptors are empty for 11 methods.** They are advertised with scope BOTH and no capabilities, callers or effects [`services.rs#L204-L212`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services.rs#L204-L212), which contradicts their method options, for example [`provenance_service.proto#L28-L35`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L28-L35). ForkSession, RebuildSessionIndex, GetProvenance, ExportProvenance and ExplainTurns are not advertised at all [`services.rs#L97-L109`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services.rs#L97-L109). A client that gates on the method matrix sees an incomplete picture.
- **ExplainTurns inputs whose effect is decided elsewhere.** `session_id` is ignored [`services_agent.rs#L5158`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5158), so the turns are the history entries of `view_name`, not the session's turns [`services_agent.rs#L4946-L4947`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4946-L4947). What an empty `view_name` or empty `model` does is decided in atomic-repository and atomic-agent [`services_agent.rs#L4947`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4947) [`services_agent.rs#L5034`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5034).
- **DispatchTurnEvent inputs and clock.** Empty `agent_id` or `session_id` are passed unchecked into the orchestrator [`services_agent.rs#L4507`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4507) [`services_agent.rs#L4557`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4557), whose behaviour is in atomic-agent. The server-clock fallback for an absent `timestamp` [`services_agent.rs#L4563-L4567`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4563-L4567) conflicts with the caller-supplied time rule [`common.proto#L18-L22`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/common/common.proto#L18-L22).
- **ForkSession retries.** There is no request-ID replay [`services_agent.rs#L1233-L1239`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L1233-L1239). What a repeated fork with the same `forked_session_id` does is decided in atomic-repository [`services_agent.rs#L4800-L4802`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4800-L4802).
- **GetSession's response shape is still SHAPE-CONFIRM.** The extra session-stamp fields are marked SHAPE-CONFIRM (Slice 2) [`provenance_messages.proto#L321-L322`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L321-L322). GetSession also returns ledger turns with `provenance_id`, `generation` and `state` zeroed [`services_query.rs#L699-L713`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_query.rs#L699-L713), so they cannot be fed into the journal RPCs.
- **ExportProvenance's `prov_jsonld` is never produced.** The proto calls it the W3C PROV projection [`provenance_messages.proto#L400`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L400), but it is always empty [`services_agent.rs#L5352`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5352), and the doc comment says the CLI projects on the client [`services_agent.rs#L5244-L5248`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5244-L5248). It is unclear whether the field will ever be filled or should be treated as retired.
- **The order of `prior_activities`.** The proto says the chain is oldest first [`provenance_messages.proto#L418-L420`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_messages.proto#L418-L420). The handler appends the immediate predecessor first and walks back from there [`services_agent.rs#L5310-L5323`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L5310-L5323), so the list is newest first. Which order the client renderer expects is not shown in libatomic.
- **Which seams PrepareCheckpoint owns.** The proto comment on PrepareCheckpoint names before-envelope-commit and after-envelope-commit among its seams [`provenance_service.proto#L49-L51`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L49-L51). The code trips both failpoints inside AppendEnvelopes [`provenance_core.rs#L130-L134`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L130-L134), and only after-checkpoint-prepare inside PrepareCheckpoint [`provenance_core.rs#L167`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/provenance_core.rs#L167).
- **ListSessions ordering.** The proto says newest activity first [`provenance_service.proto#L174`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L174). The handler keeps the order returned by `list_session_ledgers` [`services_agent.rs#L4708`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4708) [`services_agent.rs#L4738-L4740`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4738-L4740), which atomic-repository decides.
- **Reads take the exclusive gate, and GetSession and ListSessions open the repository writable.** The README says reads run as concurrent read transactions [`README.md#L114-L117`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/README.md?plain=1#L114-L117). The handlers serialize every request [`state.rs#L36-L40`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L36-L40), and GetSession and ListSessions open the writable repository [`services_query.rs#L595`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_query.rs#L595) [`services_agent.rs#L4707`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/services_agent.rs#L4707) [`state.rs#L100-L110`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/src/daemon/state.rs#L100-L110), although both declare only `RPC_EFFECT_REPOSITORY_READ` [`provenance_service.proto#L119-L126`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L119-L126) [`provenance_service.proto#L197-L203`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L197-L203).
- **A misplaced proto comment.** The comment at [`provenance_service.proto#L174-L175`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L174-L175) describes ListSessions ("Recent session ledgers ... Add-only sibling of GetSession") but sits directly above ExplainTurns' comment and declaration [`provenance_service.proto#L176-L185`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L176-L185).
- **The proto README and COVERAGE.md lists are out of date.** COVERAGE.md §1 lists 13 ProvenanceService RPCs [`COVERAGE.md#L31`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/COVERAGE.md?plain=1#L31). The service declares 18; the five not listed are AppendHookLog, LoadHookLog, DispatchTurnEvent, ExplainTurns and ListSessions [`provenance_service.proto#L14`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L14) [`provenance_service.proto#L20`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L20) [`provenance_service.proto#L167`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L167) [`provenance_service.proto#L185`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L185) [`provenance_service.proto#L197`](https://github.com/atomicdotdev/atomic/blob/a6c3e359456e83cf5a967a2e8265f11e0ba45d80/libatomic/proto/atomic/services/provenance/provenance_service.proto#L197).
